    15→// GetSessionLog returns the session transcript and session ID.
    16→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetSessionLog(commitHash string) ([]byte, string, error) {
    17→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    18→	if err != nil {
    19→		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    20→	}
    21→
    22→	// Try to look up by checkpoint ID first (12 hex chars)
    23→	if len(commitHash) == 12 {
    24→		log, err := s.getCheckpointLog(commitHash)
    25→		if err == nil {
    26→			// Find session ID from metadata (best-effort lookup)
    27→			checkpoints, _ := s.listCheckpoints() //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort session ID lookup
    28→			for _, cp := range checkpoints {
    29→				if cp.CheckpointID == commitHash {
    30→					return log, cp.SessionID, nil
    31→				}
    32→			}
    33→			return log, "", nil
    34→		}
    35→	}
    36→
    37→	// Try to look up by commit
    38→	hash, err := repo.ResolveRevision(plumbing.Revision(commitHash))
    39→	if err == nil {
    40→		commit, err := repo.CommitObject(*hash)
    41→		if err == nil {
    42→			// Check for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
    43→			checkpointID, hasTrailer := paths.ParseCheckpointTrailer(commit.Message)
    44→			if hasTrailer && checkpointID != "" {
    45→				log, logErr := s.getCheckpointLog(checkpointID)
    46→				if logErr == nil {
    47→					// Find session ID
    48→					checkpoints, _ := s.listCheckpoints() //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort session ID lookup
    49→					for _, cp := range checkpoints {
    50→						if cp.CheckpointID == checkpointID {
    51→							return log, cp.SessionID, nil
    52→						}
    53→					}
    54→					return log, "", nil
    55→				}
    56→			}
    57→
    58→			// Fall back to Entire-Session trailer
    59→			sessionID, found := paths.ParseSessionTrailer(commit.Message)
    60→			if found {
    61→				log, err := s.getSessionLogBySessionID(sessionID)
    62→				if err == nil {
    63→					return log, sessionID, nil
    64→				}
    65→			}
    66→
    67→			// Try metadata trailer (shadow branch commit)
    68→			metadataDir, found := paths.ParseMetadataTrailer(commit.Message)
    69→			if found {
    70→				sessionID := filepath.Base(metadataDir)
    71→				tree, treeErr := commit.Tree()
    72→				if treeErr == nil {
    73→					// Try current format first, then legacy
    74→					logPath := filepath.Join(metadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileName)
    75→					if file, fileErr := tree.File(logPath); fileErr == nil {
    76→						if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
    77→							return []byte(content), sessionID, nil
    78→						}
    79→					}
    80→					logPath = filepath.Join(metadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy)
    81→					if file, fileErr := tree.File(logPath); fileErr == nil {
    82→						if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
    83→							return []byte(content), sessionID, nil
    84→						}
    85→					}
    86→				}
    87→			}
    88→		}
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Try as session ID (commitHash is actually a sessionID)
    92→	log, err := s.getSessionLogBySessionID(commitHash)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, "", err
    95→	}
    96→	return log, commitHash, nil
    97→}
    98→
    99→// getSessionLogBySessionID returns all transcripts for a session.
   100→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getSessionLogBySessionID(sessionID string) ([]byte, error) {
   101→	checkpoints, err := s.getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID)
   102→	if err != nil {
   103→		// Fall back to active session shadow branch
   104→		return s.getSessionLogFromShadow(sessionID)
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	// Sort by time (oldest first)
   108→	sort.Slice(checkpoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   109→		return checkpoints[i].CreatedAt.Before(checkpoints[j].CreatedAt)
   110→	})
   111→
   112→	var result []byte
   113→	for i, cp := range checkpoints {
   114→		log, err := s.getCheckpointLog(cp.CheckpointID)
   115→		if err != nil {
   116→			continue
   117→		}
   118→		if i > 0 {
   119→			result = append(result, []byte("\n\n--- Next commit ---\n\n")...)
   120→		}
   121→		result = append(result, log...)
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	if len(result) == 0 {
   125→		return s.getSessionLogFromShadow(sessionID)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	return result, nil
   129→}
   130→
   131→// getSessionLogFromShadow gets transcript from active shadow branch.
   132→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getSessionLogFromShadow(sessionID string) ([]byte, error) {
   133→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   139→	if err != nil || state == nil {
   140→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no active session: %s", sessionID)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit)
   144→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   145→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   146→	if err != nil {
   147→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get shadow branch reference: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   151→	if err != nil {
   152→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   156→	if err != nil {
   157→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDir(sessionID)
   161→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   162→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   163→			return []byte(content), nil
   164→		}
   165→	}
   166→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   167→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   168→			return []byte(content), nil
   169→		}
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	return nil, errors.New("no transcript found")
   173→}

   185→// GetSessionInfo returns the current session info.
   186→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetSessionInfo() (*SessionInfo, error) {
   187→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	// Check if we're on a shadow branch
   193→	head, err := repo.Head()
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	if head.Name().IsBranch() {
   199→		branchName := head.Name().Short()
   200→		if strings.HasPrefix(branchName, shadowBranchPrefix) {
   201→			return nil, ErrNoSession
   202→		}
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	// Find sessions for current HEAD
   206→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForCommit(head.Hash().String())
   207→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   208→		return nil, ErrNoSession
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// Return info for most recent session
   212→	state := sessions[0]
   213→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit)
   214→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   215→
   216→	info := &SessionInfo{
   217→		SessionID: state.SessionID,
   218→		Reference: shadowBranchName,
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	if ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err == nil {
   222→		info.CommitHash = ref.Hash().String()
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	return info, nil
   226→}
   227→
   228→// GetMetadataRef returns a reference to the metadata for the given checkpoint.
   229→// For manual-commit strategy, returns the sharded path on entire/sessions branch.
   230→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetMetadataRef(checkpoint Checkpoint) string {
   231→	if checkpoint.CheckpointID == "" {
   232→		return ""
   233→	}
   234→	return paths.MetadataBranchName + ":" + paths.CheckpointPath(checkpoint.CheckpointID)
   235→}
   236→
   237→// GetSessionMetadataRef returns a reference to the most recent metadata commit for a session.
   238→// For manual-commit strategy, metadata lives on the entire/sessions branch.
   239→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetSessionMetadataRef(_ string) string {
   240→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   241→	if err != nil {
   242→		return ""
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	// Get the sessions branch
   246→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   247→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   248→	if err != nil {
   249→		return ""
   250→	}
   251→
   252→	// The tip of entire/sessions contains all condensed sessions
   253→	// Return a reference to it (sessionID is not used as all sessions are on the same branch)
   254→	return paths.FormatSourceRefTrailer(paths.MetadataBranchName, ref.Hash().String())
   255→}
   256→
   257→// GetSessionContext returns the context.md content for a session.
   258→// For manual-commit strategy, reads from the entire/sessions branch using sharded paths.
   259→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetSessionContext(sessionID string) string {
   260→	// Find a checkpoint for this session
   261→	checkpoints, err := s.getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID)
   262→	if err != nil || len(checkpoints) == 0 {
   263→		return ""
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	// Use the most recent checkpoint
   267→	sort.Slice(checkpoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   268→		return checkpoints[i].CreatedAt.After(checkpoints[j].CreatedAt)
   269→	})
   270→	checkpointID := checkpoints[0].CheckpointID
   271→
   272→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   273→	if err != nil {
   274→		return ""
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Get the sessions branch
   278→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   279→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   280→	if err != nil {
   281→		return ""
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return ""
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		return ""
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	// Context.md is at <sharded-path>/context.md
   295→	contextPath := paths.CheckpointPath(checkpointID) + "/" + paths.ContextFileName
   296→	file, err := tree.File(contextPath)
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return ""
   299→	}
   300→	content, err := file.Contents()
   301→	if err != nil {
   302→		return ""
   303→	}
   304→	return content
   305→}
   306→
   307→// GetCheckpointLog returns the session transcript for a specific checkpoint.
   308→// For manual-commit strategy, metadata is stored at sharded paths on entire/sessions branch.
   309→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetCheckpointLog(checkpoint Checkpoint) ([]byte, error) {
   310→	if checkpoint.CheckpointID == "" {
   311→		return nil, ErrNoMetadata
   312→	}
   313→	return s.getCheckpointLog(checkpoint.CheckpointID)
   314→}
   315→
   316→// GetAdditionalSessions implements SessionSource interface.
   317→// Returns active sessions from .git/entire-sessions/ that haven't yet been condensed.
   318→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetAdditionalSessions() ([]*Session, error) {
   319→	activeStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   320→	if err != nil {
   321→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	if len(activeStates) == 0 {
   325→		return nil, nil
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	var sessions []*Session
   329→	for _, state := range activeStates {
   330→		session := &Session{
   331→			ID:          state.SessionID,
   332→			Description: NoDescription,
   333→			Strategy:    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   334→			StartTime:   state.StartedAt,
   335→		}
   336→
   337→		// Try to get description from shadow branch
   338→		if description := s.getDescriptionFromShadowBranch(state.SessionID, state.BaseCommit); description != "" {
   339→			session.Description = description
   340→		}
   341→
   342→		sessions = append(sessions, session)
   343→	}
   344→
   345→	return sessions, nil
   346→}
   347→
   348→// getDescriptionFromShadowBranch reads the session description from the shadow branch.
   349→// sessionID is expected to be an Entire session ID (already date-prefixed like "2026-01-12-abc123").
   350→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getDescriptionFromShadowBranch(sessionID, baseCommit string) string {
   351→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   352→	if err != nil {
   353→		return ""
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit)
   357→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   358→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   359→	if err != nil {
   360→		return ""
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   364→	if err != nil {
   365→		return ""
   366→	}
   367→
   368→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   369→	if err != nil {
   370→		return ""
   371→	}
   372→
   373→	// Use SessionMetadataDirFromEntireID since sessionID is already an Entire session ID
   374→	// (with date prefix like "2026-01-12-abc123")
   375→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromEntireID(sessionID)
   376→	return getSessionDescriptionFromTree(tree, metadataDir)
   377→}

> AGENT

Now I understand. Here's my proposed approach:

## Refactoring Plan

**Current state:** `GetSessionLog(commitHash)` does multiple things:
1. Tries as checkpoint ID (12 chars)
2. Resolves commit → extracts `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer → looks up
3. Falls back to `Entire-Session` trailer (you want removed)
4. Handles shadow branch `Entire-Metadata` trailer
5. Falls back to session ID lookup

**Proposed new structure:**

```go
// GetSessionLog now expects a checkpoint ID directly.
// For commit hashes, use CheckpointIDFromCommit first.
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetSessionLog(checkpointID string) ([]byte, string, error) {
    // Simple: just look up by checkpoint ID
    log, err := s.getCheckpointLog(checkpointID)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, "", err
    }
    
    // Find session ID from metadata
    checkpoints, _ := s.listCheckpoints()
    for _, cp := range checkpoints {
        if cp.CheckpointID == checkpointID {
            return log, cp.SessionID, nil
        }
    }
    return log, "", nil
}

// CheckpointIDFromCommit extracts the checkpoint ID from a commit's trailer.
// Use this before calling GetSessionLog when you have a commit reference.
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CheckpointIDFromCommit(commitRef string) (string, error) {
    repo, err := OpenRepository()
    if err != nil {
        return "", err
    }
    
    hash, err := repo.ResolveRevision(plumbing.Revision(commitRef))
    if err != nil {
        return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to resolve commit: %w", err)
    }
    
    commit, err := repo.CommitObject(*hash)
    if err != nil {
        return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
    }
    
    checkpointID, hasTrailer := paths.ParseCheckpointTrailer(commit.Message)
    if !hasTrailer || checkpointID == "" {
        return "", ErrNoMetadata
    }
    
    return checkpointID, nil
}
```

**What happens to the other cases:**

| Old logic | Where it moves |
|-----------|---------------|
| 12-char checkpoint ID | Stays in `GetSessionLog` (primary path) |
| Commit → `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer | New `CheckpointIDFromCommit` method |
| `Entire-Session` trailer | **Removed** as requested |
| `Entire-Metadata` trailer (shadow) | Could add `CheckpointIDFromShadowCommit` if needed, or callers use `GetSessionLogBySessionID` directly |
| Session ID fallback | Move to a separate `GetSessionLogBySessionID` (already exists as private) |

**Callers would change from:**
```go
content, sessionID, err := strat.GetSessionLog(commitRef)
```

**To:**
```go
// When you have a checkpoint ID directly:
content, sessionID, err := strat.GetSessionLog(checkpointID)

// When you have a commit hash:
checkpointID, err := strat.CheckpointIDFromCommit(commitRef)
if err != nil { ... }
content, sessionID, err := strat.GetSessionLog(checkpointID)
```

Do you want me to implement this refactor? I can also:
1. Export `GetSessionLogBySessionID` if you still need the session ID lookup path
2. Add a `CheckpointIDFromShadowCommit` if the `Entire-Metadata` path is still needed

> AGENT

That's a cleaner approach. Using `plumbing.Hash` as the type enforces that callers must have a real git commit, and the method has a single responsibility: extract the `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer from that commit and look up the log.

```go
// GetSessionLog returns the session transcript for a commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetSessionLog(commitHash plumbing.Hash) ([]byte, string, error) {
    repo, err := OpenRepository()
    if err != nil {
        return nil, "", err
    }
    
    commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
    }
    
    checkpointID, hasTrailer := paths.ParseCheckpointTrailer(commit.Message)
    if !hasTrailer || checkpointID == "" {
        return nil, "", ErrNoMetadata
    }
    
    log, err := s.getCheckpointLog(checkpointID)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, "", err
    }
    
    // Find session ID from metadata
    checkpoints, _ := s.listCheckpoints()
    for _, cp := range checkpoints {
        if cp.CheckpointID == checkpointID {
            return log, cp.SessionID, nil
        }
    }
    return log, "", nil
}
```

**For callers who have a checkpoint ID directly**, they can use the existing `GetCheckpointLog(Checkpoint)` at line 309, or we could add a simpler helper:

```go
// GetLogByCheckpointID returns the log for a checkpoint ID directly.
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetLogByCheckpointID(checkpointID string) ([]byte, string, error) {
    // ... same lookup logic
}
```

**Impact on callers:**

| Current call | New approach |
|-------------|--------------|
| `GetSessionLog(checkpointID)` | `GetLogByCheckpointID(checkpointID)` or `GetCheckpointLog(Checkpoint{CheckpointID: id})` |
| `GetSessionLog(commitHashString)` | Resolve to `plumbing.Hash` first, then `GetSessionLog(hash)` |

This also means the `Strategy` interface would change from `GetSessionLog(checkpointID string)` to `GetSessionLog(commitHash plumbing.Hash)`. AutoCommit would need the same update.

Does this direction work for you?

> DEVELOPER

is it worth making the CheckpointID a type?

> AGENT

Yes, I think it's worth it. You'd get:

1. **Type safety** - can't mix up checkpoint IDs with session IDs, commit hashes, branch names, etc.
2. **Self-documenting signatures** - `GetLogByCheckpointID(id CheckpointID)` vs `GetLogByCheckpointID(id string)`
3. **Validation in one place** - the 12-hex-char format can be enforced at construction

```go
// CheckpointID is a 12-character hex identifier linking commits to metadata.
type CheckpointID string

func NewCheckpointID(s string) (CheckpointID, error) {
    if len(s) != 12 {
        return "", fmt.Errorf("checkpoint ID must be 12 characters, got %d", len(s))
    }
    for _, c := range s {
        if !((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f')) {
            return "", fmt.Errorf("checkpoint ID must be lowercase hex, got %q", s)
        }
    }
    return CheckpointID(s), nil
}

// GenerateCheckpointID creates a new random checkpoint ID.
func GenerateCheckpointID() CheckpointID {
    // existing generation logic, can skip validation since we control it
}

func (id CheckpointID) String() string { return string(id) }

// Shard returns the sharded path components (first 2 chars, remaining 10).
func (id CheckpointID) Shard() (string, string) {
    return string(id[:2]), string(id[2:])
}
```

Then structs and methods become clearer:

```go
type Checkpoint struct {
    CheckpointID CheckpointID  // was string
    SessionID    string
    // ...
}

func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetLogByCheckpointID(id CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, error)
```

   300→
   301→// ParseSessionTrailer extracts the session ID from a commit message.
   302→// Returns the session ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   303→// Note: If multiple Entire-Session trailers exist, this returns only the first one.
   304→// Use ParseAllSessionTrailers to get all session IDs.
   305→func ParseSessionTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   306→	matches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   307→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   308→		return strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true
   309→	}
   310→	return "", false
   311→}
   312→
   313→// GenerateCheckpointID generates a unique 12-character hex checkpoint ID.
   314→// Uses crypto/rand for secure random generation.
   315→// Returns 12 hex characters (6 bytes = ~281 trillion unique values).
   316→func GenerateCheckpointID() string {
   317→	b := make([]byte, 6)
   318→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // crypto/rand.Read is documented to always succeed on supported platforms
   319→	rand.Read(b)
   320→	return hex.EncodeToString(b)
   321→}
   322→
   323→// ParseCheckpointTrailer extracts the checkpoint ID from a commit message.
   324→// Returns the checkpoint ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   325→func ParseCheckpointTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   326→	matches := checkpointTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   327→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   328→		return strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true
   329→	}
   330→	return "", false
   331→}
   332→
   333→// CheckpointPath returns the sharded path for a checkpoint ID on entire/sessions.
   334→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
   335→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
   336→func CheckpointPath(checkpointID string) string {
   337→	if len(checkpointID) < 3 {
   338→		return checkpointID
   339→	}
   340→	return checkpointID[:2] + "/" + checkpointID[2:]
   341→}
   342→
   343→// Pre-compiled regexes for trailer parsing (compiled at package init for performance)
   344→var (
   345→	// checkpointIDRegex matches valid checkpoint IDs (12 lowercase hex characters)
   346→	checkpointIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-f0-9]{12}$`)
   347→
   348→	// pathSafeRegex matches strings safe for use in file paths (no path separators or traversal)
   349→	pathSafeRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+$`)
   350→
   351→	// Trailer parsing regexes

   353→	metadataTrailerRegex     = regexp.MustCompile(MetadataTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   354→	taskMetadataTrailerRegex = regexp.MustCompile(MetadataTaskTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   355→	baseCommitTrailerRegex   = regexp.MustCompile(BaseCommitTrailerKey + `:\s*([a-f0-9]{40})`)

   357→	sessionTrailerRegex      = regexp.MustCompile(SessionTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   358→	checkpointTrailerRegex   = regexp.MustCompile(CheckpointTrailerKey + `:\s*([a-f0-9]+)`)
   359→)
   360→
   361→// ValidateCheckpointID validates that a checkpoint ID is exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
   362→func ValidateCheckpointID(id string) error {
   363→	if !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(id) {
   364→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", id)
   365→	}
   366→	return nil
   367→}
   368→
   369→// ValidateSessionID validates that a session ID is non-empty and doesn't contain path separators.
   370→func ValidateSessionID(id string) error {
   371→	if id == "" {
   372→		return errors.New("session ID cannot be empty")
   373→	}
   374→	if strings.ContainsAny(id, "/\\") {
   375→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID %q: contains path separators", id)
   376→	}
   377→	return nil
   378→}

    78→// repoRootCache caches the repository root to avoid repeated git commands.
    79→// The cache is keyed by the current working directory to handle directory changes.
    80→var (
    81→	repoRootMu       sync.RWMutex
    82→	repoRootCache    string
    83→	repoRootCacheDir string
    84→)
    85→
    86→// RepoRoot returns the git repository root directory.
    87→// Uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' which works from any subdirectory.
    88→// The result is cached per working directory.
    89→// Returns an error if not inside a git repository.
    90→func RepoRoot() (string, error) {
    91→	// Get current working directory to check cache validity
    92→	cwd, err := os.Getwd()
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		cwd = ""
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	// Check cache with read lock first
    98→	repoRootMu.RLock()
    99→	if repoRootCache != "" && repoRootCacheDir == cwd {
   100→		cached := repoRootCache
   101→		repoRootMu.RUnlock()
   102→		return cached, nil
   103→	}
   104→	repoRootMu.RUnlock()
   105→
   106→	// Cache miss - get repo root and update cache with write lock
   107→	ctx := context.Background()
   108→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
   109→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git repository root: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	root := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   115→
   116→	repoRootMu.Lock()
   117→	repoRootCache = root
   118→	repoRootCacheDir = cwd

   222→// FormatStrategyTrailer creates a commit message with just the strategy trailer (no metadata dir)
   223→func FormatStrategyTrailer(message, strategy string) string {
   224→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\n%s: %s\n", message, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)
   225→}
   226→
   227→// ParseStrategyTrailer extracts strategy from commit message.
   228→// Returns the strategy name and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   229→func ParseStrategyTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   230→	matches := strategyTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   231→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   232→		return NormalizeStrategyName(strings.TrimSpace(matches[1])), true
   233→	}
   234→	return "", false
   235→}
   236→
   237→// NormalizeStrategyName maps legacy strategy names to current names.
   238→// These are the strategy names used during initial development and are kept for backwards
   239→// compatibility
   240→func NormalizeStrategyName(name string) string {
   241→	switch name {
   242→	case "dual":
   243→		return "auto-commit"
   244→	case "shadow":
   245→		return "manual-commit"
   246→	default:
   247→		return name
   248→	}
   249→}

Here's what would move to a `trailers` package:

**Constants (10):**
- `MetadataTrailerKey`, `MetadataTaskTrailerKey`, `StrategyTrailerKey`
- `BaseCommitTrailerKey`, `SessionTrailerKey`, `CondensationTrailerKey`
- `SourceRefTrailerKey`, `CheckpointTrailerKey`
- `EphemeralBranchTrailerKey`, `AgentTrailerKey`

**Parse functions (8):**
- `ParseStrategyTrailer`, `ParseMetadataTrailer`, `ParseTaskMetadataTrailer`
- `ParseBaseCommitTrailer`, `ParseCondensationTrailer`, `ParseSessionTrailer`
- `ParseCheckpointTrailer`, `ParseAllSessionTrailers`

**Format functions (3):**
- `FormatStrategyTrailer`, `FormatTaskMetadataTrailer`, `FormatTaskMetadataTrailerWithStrategy`
- `FormatSourceRefTrailer`

**Utilities:**
- `NormalizeStrategyName`
- The compiled regexes

**Plus the new `CheckpointID` type and `GenerateCheckpointID`** could live there too.

`CheckpointPath` could stay in `paths` (it's path-related) or become a method on `CheckpointID`.

`MetadataBranchName` is a branch name, not a trailer - maybe stays in `paths`.

This is a clean split - trailers are all about commit message parsing/formatting. Want me to proceed with creating the `trailers` package?

  "content": "// Package trailers provides parsing and formatting for Entire commit message trailers.\n// Trailers are key-value metadata appended to git commit messages following the\n// git trailer convention (key: value format after a blank line).\npackage trailers\n\nimport (\n\t\"crypto/rand\"\n\t\"encoding/hex\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\n// Trailer key constants used in commit messages.\nconst (\n\t// MetadataTrailerKey points to the metadata directory within a commit tree.\n\tMetadataTrailerKey = \"Entire-Metadata\"\n\n\t// MetadataTaskTrailerKey points to the task metadata directory for subagent checkpoints.\n\tMetadataTaskTrailerKey = \"Entire-Metadata-Task\"\n\n\t// StrategyTrailerKey indicates which strategy created the commit.\n\tStrategyTrailerKey = \"Entire-Strategy\"\n\n\t// BaseCommitTrailerKey links shadow commits to their base code commit.\n\tBaseCommitTrailerKey = \"Base-Commit\"\n\n\t// SessionTrailerKey identifies which session created a commit.\n\tSessionTrailerKey = \"Entire-Session\"\n\n\t// CondensationTrailerKey identifies the condensation ID for a commit (legacy).\n\tCondensationTrailerKey = \"Entire-Condensation\"\n\n\t// SourceRefTrailerKey links code commits to their metadata on a shadow/metadata branch.\n\t// Format: \"<branch>@<commit-hash>\" e.g. \"entire/metadata@abc123def456\"\n\tSourceRefTrailerKey = \"Entire-Source-Ref\"\n\n\t// CheckpointTrailerKey links commits to their checkpoint metadata on entire/sessions.\n\t// Format: 12 hex characters e.g. \"a3b2c4d5e6f7\"\n\t// This trailer survives git amend and rebase operations.\n\tCheckpointTrailerKey = \"Entire-Checkpoint\"\n\n\t// EphemeralBranchTrailerKey identifies the shadow branch that a checkpoint originated from.\n\t// Used in manual-commit strategy checkpoint commits on entire/sessions branch.\n\t// Format: full branch name e.g. \"entire/2b4c177\"\n\tEphemeralBranchTrailerKey = \"Ephemeral-branch\"\n\n\t// AgentTrailerKey identifies the agent that created a checkpoint.\n\t// Format: human-readable agent name e.g. \"Claude Code\", \"Cursor\"\n\tAgentTrailerKey = \"Entire-Agent\"\n)\n\n// Pre-compiled regexes for trailer parsing.\nvar (\n\t// checkpointIDRegex matches valid checkpoint IDs (12 lowercase hex characters).\n\tcheckpointIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-f0-9]{12}$`)\n\n\t// Trailer parsing regexes.\n\tstrategyTrailerRegex     = regexp.MustCompile(StrategyTrailerKey + `:\\s*(.+)`)\n\tmetadataTrailerRegex     = regexp.MustCompile(MetadataTrailerKey + `:\\s*(.+)`)\n\ttaskMetadataTrailerRegex = regexp.MustCompile(MetadataTaskTrailerKey + `:\\s*(.+)`)\n\tbaseCommitTrailerRegex   = regexp.MustCompile(BaseCommitTrailerKey + `:\\s*([a-f0-9]{40})`)\n\tcondensationTrailerRegex = regexp.MustCompile(CondensationTrailerKey + `:\\s*(.+)`)\n\tsessionTrailerRegex      = regexp.MustCompile(SessionTrailerKey + `:\\s*(.+)`)\n\tcheckpointTrailerRegex   = regexp.MustCompile(CheckpointTrailerKey + `:\\s*([a-f0-9]+)`)\n)\n\n// CheckpointID is a 12-character lowercase hex identifier linking commits to their metadata.\n// It is generated randomly and used as the stable identifier for checkpoint data on the\n// entire/sessions branch. The format supports ~281 trillion unique values (6 bytes).\ntype CheckpointID string\n\n// EmptyCheckpointID represents an unset or invalid checkpoint ID.\nconst EmptyCheckpointID CheckpointID = \"\"\n\n// NewCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, validating the format.\n// Returns an error if the string is not exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.\nfunc NewCheckpointID(s string) (CheckpointID, error) {\n\tif !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(s) {\n\t\treturn EmptyCheckpointID, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters\", s)\n\t}\n\treturn CheckpointID(s), nil\n}\n\n// MustCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, panicking if invalid.\n// Use only when the ID is known to be valid (e.g., from GenerateCheckpointID).\nfunc MustCheckpointID(s string) CheckpointID {\n\tid, err := NewCheckpointID(s)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tpanic(err)\n\t}\n\treturn id\n}\n\n// GenerateCheckpointID generates a new random CheckpointID.\n// Uses crypto/rand for secure random generation.\n// Returns 12 hex characters (6 bytes = ~281 trillion unique values).\nfunc GenerateCheckpointID() CheckpointID {\n\tb := make([]byte, 6)\n\t//nolint:errcheck,gosec // crypto/rand.Read is documented to always succeed on supported platforms\n\trand.Read(b)\n\treturn CheckpointID(hex.EncodeToString(b))\n}\n\n// String returns the checkpoint ID as a string.\nfunc (id CheckpointID) String() string {\n\treturn string(id)\n}\n\n// IsEmpty returns true if the checkpoint ID is empty or unset.\nfunc (id CheckpointID) IsEmpty() bool {\n\treturn id == EmptyCheckpointID\n}\n\n// Path returns the sharded path for this checkpoint ID on entire/sessions.\n// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.\n// Example: \"a3b2c4d5e6f7\" -> \"a3/b2c4d5e6f7\"\nfunc (id CheckpointID) Path() string {\n\tif len(id) < 3 {\n\t\treturn string(id)\n\t}\n\treturn string(id[:2]) + \"/\" + string(id[2:])\n}\n\n// ValidateCheckpointID validates that a string is a valid checkpoint ID format.\n// Returns an error if invalid, nil if valid.\nfunc ValidateCheckpointID(id string) error {\n\tif !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(id) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters\", id)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ParseStrategy extracts strategy from commit message.\n// Returns the strategy name and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseStrategy(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := strategyTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn NormalizeStrategyName(strings.TrimSpace(matches[1])), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseMetadata extracts metadata dir from commit message.\n// Returns the metadata directory and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseMetadata(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := metadataTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseTaskMetadata extracts task metadata dir from commit message.\n// Returns the task metadata directory and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseTaskMetadata(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := taskMetadataTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseBaseCommit extracts the base commit SHA from a commit message.\n// Returns the full SHA and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseBaseCommit(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := baseCommitTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn matches[1], true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseCondensation extracts the condensation ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the condensation ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseCondensation(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := condensationTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseSession extracts the session ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the session ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\n// Note: If multiple Entire-Session trailers exist, this returns only the first one.\n// Use ParseAllSessions to get all session IDs.\nfunc ParseSession(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseCheckpoint extracts the checkpoint ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the CheckpointID and true if found, EmptyCheckpointID and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseCheckpoint(commitMessage string) (CheckpointID, bool) {\n\tmatches := checkpointTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\tidStr := strings.TrimSpace(matches[1])\n\t\t// Validate it's a proper checkpoint ID\n\t\tif id, err := NewCheckpointID(idStr); err == nil {\n\t\t\treturn id, true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn EmptyCheckpointID, false\n}\n\n// ParseAllSessions extracts all session IDs from a commit message.\n// Returns a slice of session IDs (may be empty if none found).\n// This is useful for commits that may have multiple Entire-Session trailers.\nfunc ParseAllSessions(commitMessage string) []string {\n\tmatches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindAllStringSubmatch(commitMessage, -1)\n\tif len(matches) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tsessionIDs := make([]string, 0, len(matches))\n\tfor _, match := range matches {\n\t\tif len(match) > 1 {\n\t\t\tsessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, strings.TrimSpace(match[1]))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn sessionIDs\n}\n\n// NormalizeStrategyName maps legacy strategy names to current names.\n// These are the strategy names used during initial development and are kept for backwards\n// compatibility.\nfunc NormalizeStrategyName(name string) string {\n\tswitch name {\n\tcase \"dual\":\n\t\treturn \"auto-commit\"\n\tcase \"shadow\":\n\t\treturn \"manual-commit\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn name\n\t}\n}\n\n// FormatStrategy creates a commit message with just the strategy trailer.\nfunc FormatStrategy(message, strategy string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n%s: %s\\n\", message, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)\n}\n\n// FormatTaskMetadata creates a commit message with task metadata trailer.\nfunc FormatTaskMetadata(message, taskMetadataDir string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n%s: %s\\n\", message, MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataDir)\n}\n\n// FormatTaskMetadataWithStrategy creates a commit message with task metadata and strategy trailers.\nfunc FormatTaskMetadataWithStrategy(message, taskMetadataDir, strategy string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n%s: %s\\n%s: %s\\n\", message, MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataDir, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)\n}\n\n// FormatSourceRef creates a formatted source ref string for the trailer.\n// Format: \"<branch>@<commit-hash>\"\nfunc FormatSourceRef(branch, commitHash string) string {\n\treturn branch + \"@\" + commitHash\n}\n"

    78→// repoRootCache caches the repository root to avoid repeated git commands.
    79→// The cache is keyed by the current working directory to handle directory changes.
    80→var (
    81→	repoRootMu       sync.RWMutex
    82→	repoRootCache    string
    83→	repoRootCacheDir string
    84→)
    85→
    86→// RepoRoot returns the git repository root directory.
    87→// Uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' which works from any subdirectory.
    88→// The result is cached per working directory.
    89→// Returns an error if not inside a git repository.
    90→func RepoRoot() (string, error) {
    91→	// Get current working directory to check cache validity
    92→	cwd, err := os.Getwd()
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		cwd = ""
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	// Check cache with read lock first
    98→	repoRootMu.RLock()
    99→	if repoRootCache != "" && repoRootCacheDir == cwd {
   100→		cached := repoRootCache
   101→		repoRootMu.RUnlock()
   102→		return cached, nil
   103→	}
   104→	repoRootMu.RUnlock()
   105→
   106→	// Cache miss - get repo root and update cache with write lock
   107→	ctx := context.Background()
   108→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
   109→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git repository root: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	root := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   115→
   116→	repoRootMu.Lock()
   117→	repoRootCache = root
   118→	repoRootCacheDir = cwd
   119→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
   120→
   121→	return root, nil
   122→}
   123→
   124→// ClearRepoRootCache clears the cached repository root.
   125→// This is primarily useful for testing when changing directories.
   126→func ClearRepoRootCache() {
   127→	repoRootMu.Lock()
   128→	repoRootCache = ""
   129→	repoRootCacheDir = ""
   130→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
   131→}
   132→
   133→// RepoRootOr returns the git repository root directory, or the current directory
   134→// if not inside a git repository. This is useful for functions that need a fallback.
   135→func RepoRootOr(fallback string) string {
   136→	root, err := RepoRoot()
   137→	if err != nil {
   138→		return fallback
   139→	}
   140→	return root
   141→}
   142→
   143→// AbsPath returns the absolute path for a relative path within the repository.
   144→// If the path is already absolute, it is returned as-is.
   145→// Uses RepoRoot() to resolve paths relative to the repository root.
   146→func AbsPath(relPath string) (string, error) {
   147→	if filepath.IsAbs(relPath) {
   148→		return relPath, nil
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	root, err := RepoRoot()
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		return "", err
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	return filepath.Join(root, relPath), nil
   157→}

   271→// ParseTaskMetadataTrailer extracts task metadata dir from commit message.
   272→// Returns the task metadata directory and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   273→func ParseTaskMetadataTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   274→	matches := taskMetadataTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   275→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   276→		return strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true
   277→	}
   278→	return "", false
   279→}
   280→
   281→// ParseBaseCommitTrailer extracts the base commit SHA from a commit message.
   282→// Returns the full SHA and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   283→func ParseBaseCommitTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   284→	matches := baseCommitTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   285→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   286→		return matches[1], true
   287→	}
   288→	return "", false
   289→}
   290→
   291→// ParseCondensationTrailer extracts the condensation ID from a commit message.
   292→// Returns the condensation ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   293→func ParseCondensationTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   294→	matches := condensationTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   295→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   296→		return strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true
   297→	}
   298→	return "", false
   299→}
   300→
   301→// ParseSessionTrailer extracts the session ID from a commit message.
   302→// Returns the session ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   303→// Note: If multiple Entire-Session trailers exist, this returns only the first one.
   304→// Use ParseAllSessionTrailers to get all session IDs.
   305→func ParseSessionTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   306→	matches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   307→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   308→		return strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true
   309→	}
   310→	return "", false
   311→}
   312→
   313→// GenerateCheckpointID generates a unique 12-character hex checkpoint ID.
   314→// Uses crypto/rand for secure random generation.
   315→// Returns 12 hex characters (6 bytes = ~281 trillion unique values).
   316→func GenerateCheckpointID() string {
   317→	b := make([]byte, 6)
   318→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // crypto/rand.Read is documented to always succeed on supported platforms
   319→	rand.Read(b)
   320→	return hex.EncodeToString(b)
   321→}
   322→
   323→// ParseCheckpointTrailer extracts the checkpoint ID from a commit message.
   324→// Returns the checkpoint ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   325→func ParseCheckpointTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   326→	matches := checkpointTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   327→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   328→		return strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true
   329→	}
   330→	return "", false
   331→}
   332→
   333→// CheckpointPath returns the sharded path for a checkpoint ID on entire/sessions.
   334→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
   335→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
   336→func CheckpointPath(checkpointID string) string {
   337→	if len(checkpointID) < 3 {
   338→		return checkpointID
   339→	}
   340→	return checkpointID[:2] + "/" + checkpointID[2:]
   341→}
   342→
   343→// Pre-compiled regexes for trailer parsing (compiled at package init for performance)
   344→var (
   345→	// checkpointIDRegex matches valid checkpoint IDs (12 lowercase hex characters)
   346→	checkpointIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-f0-9]{12}$`)
   347→
   348→	// pathSafeRegex matches strings safe for use in file paths (no path separators or traversal)
   349→	pathSafeRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+$`)
   350→
   351→	// Trailer parsing regexes
   352→	strategyTrailerRegex     = regexp.MustCompile(StrategyTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   353→	metadataTrailerRegex     = regexp.MustCompile(MetadataTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   354→	taskMetadataTrailerRegex = regexp.MustCompile(MetadataTaskTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   355→	baseCommitTrailerRegex   = regexp.MustCompile(BaseCommitTrailerKey + `:\s*([a-f0-9]{40})`)
   356→	condensationTrailerRegex = regexp.MustCompile(CondensationTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   357→	sessionTrailerRegex      = regexp.MustCompile(SessionTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   358→	checkpointTrailerRegex   = regexp.MustCompile(CheckpointTrailerKey + `:\s*([a-f0-9]+)`)
   359→)
   360→
   361→// ValidateCheckpointID validates that a checkpoint ID is exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
   362→func ValidateCheckpointID(id string) error {
   363→	if !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(id) {
   364→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", id)
   365→	}
   366→	return nil
   367→}
   368→
   369→// ValidateSessionID validates that a session ID is non-empty and doesn't contain path separators.
   370→func ValidateSessionID(id string) error {
   371→	if id == "" {
   372→		return errors.New("session ID cannot be empty")
   373→	}
   374→	if strings.ContainsAny(id, "/\\") {
   375→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID %q: contains path separators", id)
   376→	}
   377→	return nil
   378→}
   379→
   380→// ValidateToolUseID validates that a tool use ID contains only safe characters for paths.
   381→// Tool use IDs can be UUIDs or prefixed identifiers like "toolu_xxx".
   382→func ValidateToolUseID(id string) error {
   383→	if id == "" {
   384→		return nil // Empty is allowed (optional field)
   385→	}
   386→	if !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {
   387→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid tool use ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only", id)
   388→	}
   389→	return nil
   390→}
   391→
   392→// ValidateAgentID validates that an agent ID contains only safe characters for paths.
   393→func ValidateAgentID(id string) error {
   394→	if id == "" {
   395→		return nil // Empty is allowed (optional field)
   396→	}
   397→	if !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {
   398→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid agent ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only", id)
   399→	}
   400→	return nil
   401→}
   402→
   403→// TODO: THIS SHOULD BE REPLACED WITH ParseCheckpointTrailer ?!
   404→// ParseAllSessionTrailers extracts all session IDs from a commit message.
   405→// A commit can have multiple Entire-Session trailers if multiple sessions
   406→// contributed to it. Returns a slice of session IDs (may be empty).
   407→func ParseAllSessionTrailers(commitMessage string) []string {
   408→	matches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindAllStringSubmatch(commitMessage, -1)
   409→
   410→	var sessionIDs []string
   411→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   412→
   413→	for _, match := range matches {
   414→		if len(match) > 1 {
   415→			sessionID := strings.TrimSpace(match[1])
   416→			// Deduplicate
   417→			if !seen[sessionID] {
   418→				seen[sessionID] = true
   419→				sessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, sessionID)
   420→			}
   421→		}
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	return sessionIDs
   425→}
   426→
   427→// nonAlphanumericRegex matches any non-alphanumeric character
   428→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   429→
   430→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   431→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   432→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   433→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   434→}
   435→
   436→// GetClaudeProjectDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts
   437→// for the given repository path.
   438→//
   439→// In test environments, set ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR to override the default location.
   440→func GetClaudeProjectDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   441→	override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR")
   442→	if override != "" {
   443→		return override, nil
   444→	}
   445→
   446→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   447→	if err != nil {
   448→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   449→	}
   450→
   451→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   452→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   453→}
   454→
   455→// EntireSessionID generates the full Entire session ID from a Claude session ID.
   456→// The format is: YYYY-MM-DD-<claude-session-id>
   457→func EntireSessionID(claudeSessionID string) string {
   458→	return time.Now().Format("2006-01-02") + "-" + claudeSessionID
   459→}
   460→
   461→// ModelSessionID extracts the Claude session ID from an Entire session ID.
   462→// The Entire session ID format is: YYYY-MM-DD-<claude-session-id>
   463→// Returns the original string if it doesn't match the expected format.
   464→func ModelSessionID(entireSessionID string) string {
   465→	// Expected format: YYYY-MM-DD-<model-session-id> (11 chars prefix: "2025-12-02-")
   466→	if len(entireSessionID) > 11 && entireSessionID[4] == '-' && entireSessionID[7] == '-' && entireSessionID[10] == '-' {
   467→		return entireSessionID[11:]
   468→	}
   469→	// Return as-is if not in expected format (backwards compatibility)
   470→	return entireSessionID
   471→}
   472→
   473→// SessionMetadataDir returns the path to a session's metadata directory.
   474→// Takes a raw Claude session ID and adds the date prefix automatically.
   475→func SessionMetadataDir(claudeSessionID string) string {
   476→	return EntireMetadataDir + "/" + EntireSessionID(claudeSessionID)
   477→}
   478→
   479→// SessionMetadataDirFromEntireID returns the path to a session's metadata directory.
   480→// Takes an Entire session ID (already date-prefixed) without adding another prefix.
   481→func SessionMetadataDirFromEntireID(entireSessionID string) string {
   482→	return EntireMetadataDir + "/" + entireSessionID
   483→}
   484→
   485→// ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath attempts to extract a session ID from a transcript path.
   486→// Claude transcripts are stored at ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   487→// If the path doesn't match expected format, returns empty string.
   488→func ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(transcriptPath string) string {
   489→	// Try to extract from typical path: ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   490→	parts := strings.Split(filepath.ToSlash(transcriptPath), "/")
   491→	for i, part := range parts {
   492→		if part == "sessions" && i+1 < len(parts) {
   493→			// Return filename without extension
   494→			filename := parts[i+1]
   495→			if strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".jsonl") {
   496→				return strings.TrimSuffix(filename, ".jsonl")
   497→			}
   498→			return filename
   499→		}
   500→	}
   501→	return ""
   502→}
   503→
   504→// ReadCurrentSession reads the current session ID from .entire/current_session.
   505→// Returns an empty string (not error) if the file doesn't exist.
   506→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   507→func ReadCurrentSession() (string, error) {
   508→	sessionFile, err := AbsPath(CurrentSessionFile)
   509→	if err != nil {
   510→		// Fallback to relative path if not in a git repo
   511→		sessionFile = CurrentSessionFile
   512→	}
   513→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionFile) //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
   514→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   515→		return "", nil
   516→	}
   517→	if err != nil {
   518→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read current session file: %w", err)
   519→	}
   520→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(data)), nil
   521→}
   522→
   523→// WriteCurrentSession writes the session ID to .entire/current_session.
   524→// Creates the .entire directory if it doesn't exist.
   525→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   526→func WriteCurrentSession(sessionID string) error {
   527→	// Get absolute paths for the directory and file
   528→	entireDirAbs, err := AbsPath(EntireDir)
   529→	if err != nil {
   530→		// Fallback to relative path if not in a git repo
   531→		entireDirAbs = EntireDir
   532→	}
   533→	sessionFileAbs, err := AbsPath(CurrentSessionFile)
   534→	if err != nil {
   535→		sessionFileAbs = CurrentSessionFile
   536→	}
   537→
   538→	// Ensure .entire directory exists
   539→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   540→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   541→	}
   542→
   543→	// Write session ID to file (no newline, just the ID)
   544→	if err := os.WriteFile(sessionFileAbs, []byte(sessionID), 0o600); err != nil {
   545→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write current session file: %w", err)
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	return nil
   549→}

ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the condensation ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseCondensationTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := condensationTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseSessionTrailer extracts the session ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the session ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\n// Note: If multiple Entire-Session trailers exist, this returns only the first one.\n// Use ParseAllSessionTrailers to get all session IDs.\nfunc ParseSessionTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// GenerateCheckpointID generates a unique 12-character hex checkpoint ID.\n// Uses crypto/rand for secure random generation.\n// Returns 12 hex characters (6 bytes = ~281 trillion unique values).\nfunc GenerateCheckpointID() string {\n\tb := make([]byte, 6)\n\t//nolint:errcheck,gosec // crypto/rand.Read is documented to always succeed on supported platforms\n\trand.Read(b)\n\treturn hex.EncodeToString(b)\n}\n\n// ParseCheckpointTrailer extracts the checkpoint ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the checkpoint ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseCheckpointTrailer(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches :=     73→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil

    98→
    99→	return nil
   100→}

    54→func newSessionRawCmd() *cobra.Command {
    55→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    56→		Use:   "raw <commit>",
    57→		Short: "Output raw session transcript",
    58→		Long:  "Output the session transcript for a session by commit hash",
    59→		Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    60→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    61→			return runSessionRaw(args[0])
    62→		},
    63→	}
    64→

    67→
    68→func runSessionRaw(commitRef string) error {
    69→	start := GetStrategy()
    70→	// First try to get the log directly via the strategy
    71→	content, _, err := start.GetSessionLog(commitRef)
    72→	if err == nil {
    73→		fmt.Print(string(content))
    74→		return nil
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// If that fails, try to resolve through source ref trailers
    78→	if !errors.Is(err, strategy.ErrNoMetadata) {
    79→		return err
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	// Try to find session via trailers
    83→	repo, err := openRepository()
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	hash, err := repo.ResolveRevision(plumbing.Revision(commitRef))
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to resolve %s: %w", commitRef, err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(*hash)
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	_, sourceRef := parseSessionTrailers(commit.Message)
    99→	if sourceRef != "" {
   100→		sourceCommit := extractSourceCommit(sourceRef)
   101→		if sourceCommit != "" {
   102→			content, _, err := start.GetSessionLog(sourceCommit)
   103→			if err != nil {
   104→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session log: %w", err)
   105→			}
   106→			fmt.Print(string(content))
   107→			return nil
   108→		}
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	return fmt.Errorf("commit %s has no session metadata", commitRef)
   112→}
   113→
   114→// parseSessionTrailers extracts Entire-Session and Entire-Source-Ref from commit message
   115→func parseSessionTrailers(message string) (sessionID, sourceRef string) {
   116→	sessionRe := regexp.MustCompile(paths.SessionTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   117→	sourceRe := regexp.MustCompile(paths.SourceRefTrailerKey + `:\s*(.+)`)
   118→
   119→	if matches := sessionRe.FindStringSubmatch(message); len(matches) > 1 {
   120→		sessionID = strings.TrimSpace(matches[1])
   121→	}
   122→	if matches := sourceRe.FindStringSubmatch(message); len(matches) > 1 {
   123→		sourceRef = strings.TrimSpace(matches[1])
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	return sessionID, sourceRef
   127→}
   128→
   129→// extractSourceCommit extracts the commit hash from a source ref (e.g., "entire/abc1234@def567890123")
   130→func extractSourceCommit(sourceRef string) string {
   131→	parts := strings.Split(sourceRef, "@")
   132→	if len(parts) == 2 {
   133→		return parts[1]
   134→	}
   135→	return ""
   136→}

   183→func runSessionList() error {
   184→	sessions, err := strategy.ListSessions()
   185→	if err != nil {
   186→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list sessions: %w", err)
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	if len(sessions) == 0 {
   190→		fmt.Println("No sessions found.")
   191→		return nil
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Sort sessions by most recent activity (descending)
   195→	// Falls back to session ID (which contains date) when timestamps are equal
   196→	sort.Slice(sessions, func(i, j int) bool {
   197→		// Get most recent timestamp for each session
   198→		iTime := sessions[i].StartTime
   199→		for _, cp := range sessions[i].Checkpoints {
   200→			if cp.Timestamp.After(iTime) {
   201→				iTime = cp.Timestamp
   202→			}
   203→		}
   204→		jTime := sessions[j].StartTime
   205→		for _, cp := range sessions[j].Checkpoints {
   206→			if cp.Timestamp.After(jTime) {
   207→				jTime = cp.Timestamp
   208→			}
   209→		}
   210→		// If timestamps are equal, sort by session ID descending (newer dates first)
   211→		if iTime.Equal(jTime) {
   212→			return sessions[i].ID > sessions[j].ID
   213→		}
   214→		return iTime.After(jTime) // Most recent first
   215→	})
   216→
   217→	// Get current session ID for marking (ignore error, empty string is fine)
   218→	currentSessionID, readErr := paths.ReadCurrentSession()
   219→	if readErr != nil {
   220→		currentSessionID = ""
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	// Print header (2-space indent to align with marker column)
   224→	fmt.Printf("  %-19s  %-11s  %s\n", "session-id", "Checkpoints", "Description")
   225→	fmt.Printf("  %-19s  %-11s  %s\n", "───────────────────", "───────────", "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────")
   226→
   227→	for _, sess := range sessions {
   228→		// Show session ID - truncate to 19 chars for display
   229→		// This preserves enough for prefix matching (e.g., "2025-12-01-8f76b0e8")
   230→		displayID := sess.ID
   231→		if len(displayID) > 19 {
   232→			displayID = displayID[:19]
   233→		}
   234→
   235→		// Mark current session
   236→		marker := "  "
   237→		if currentSessionID != "" && (sess.ID == currentSessionID || strings.HasPrefix(sess.ID, currentSessionID)) {
   238→			marker = "* "
   239→		}
   240→
   241→		// Checkpoint count
   242→		checkpoints := len(sess.Checkpoints)
   243→
   244→		// Truncate description for display
   245→		description := sess.Description
   246→		if len(description) > 64 {
   247→			description = description[:61] + "..."
   248→		}
   249→
   250→		fmt.Printf("%s%-19s  %-11d  %s\n", marker, displayID, checkpoints, description)
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Print usage hint
   254→	fmt.Println()
   255→	fmt.Println("Resume a session: entire session resume <session-id>")
   256→
   257→	return nil
   258→}
   259→
   260→func runSessionResume(sessionID string) error {
   261→	// Get the agent for resume command formatting
   262→	ag, err := agent.Detect()
   263→	if err != nil {
   264→		ag = agent.Default()
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	strat := GetStrategy()
   268→
   269→	// Verify session exists
   270→	session, err := strategy.GetSession(sessionID)
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		if errors.Is(err, strategy.ErrNoSession) {
   273→			return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   274→		}
   275→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find session: %w", err)
   276→	}
   277→
   278→	// Check if session is already active (ignore error - empty string is fine if no current session)
   279→	currentSessionID, _ := paths.ReadCurrentSession() //nolint:errcheck // Empty string is acceptable if no current session
   280→	if currentSessionID == session.ID {
   281→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session already active: %s\n", session.ID)
   282→		agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(session.ID)
   283→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")
   284→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSessionID))
   285→		return nil
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	// Write the full session ID to current_session file
   289→	if err := paths.WriteCurrentSession(session.ID); err != nil {
   290→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set current session: %w", err)
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	// Find the most recent checkpoint for this session to get the session log
   294→	var checkpointID string
   295→	if len(session.Checkpoints) > 0 {
   296→		// Get the most recent checkpoint
   297→		checkpointID = session.Checkpoints[len(session.Checkpoints)-1].CheckpointID
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Restore agent session if we have a checkpoint
   301→	if checkpointID != "" {
   302→		if err := restoreAgentSession(ag, session.ID, checkpointID, strat); err != nil {
   303→			// Non-fatal: session is set, but memory restoration failed
   304→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: could not restore session: %v\n", err)
   305→		}
   306→	}
   307→
   308→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session resumed: %s\n", session.ID)
   309→	agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(session.ID)
   310→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")
   311→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSessionID))
   312→
   313→	return nil
   314→}
   315→
   316→// restoreAgentSession restores the session transcript using the agent abstraction.
   317→// This is agent-agnostic and works with any registered agent.
   318→func restoreAgentSession(ag agent.Agent, sessionID, checkpointID string, strat strategy.Strategy) error {
   319→	// Get repo root for session directory lookup
   320→	// Use repo root instead of CWD because Claude stores sessions per-repo,
   321→	// and running from a subdirectory would look up the wrong session directory
   322→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   323→	if err != nil {
   324→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	sessionDir, err := ag.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)
   328→	if err != nil {
   329→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to determine session directory: %w", err)
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// Extract agent-specific session ID from Entire session ID
   333→	agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
   334→	sessionLogPath := filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   335→
   336→	// Check if session log already exists
   337→	if fileExists(sessionLogPath) {
   338→		return nil // Already restored
   339→	}
   340→
   341→	// Get session log content
   342→	logContent, _, err := strat.GetSessionLog(checkpointID)
   343→	if err != nil {
   344→		if errors.Is(err, strategy.ErrNoMetadata) {
   345→			return nil // No metadata available, skip restoration
   346→		}
   347→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session log: %w", err)
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Create an AgentSession with the native data
   351→	agentSession := &agent.AgentSession{
   352→		SessionID:  agentSessionID,
   353→		AgentName:  ag.Name(),
   354→		RepoPath:   repoRoot,
   355→		SessionRef: sessionLogPath,
   356→		NativeData: logContent,
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	// Create directory if it doesn't exist
   360→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDir, 0o700); err != nil {
   361→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	// Write the session using the agent's WriteSession method
   365→	if err := ag.WriteSession(agentSession); err != nil {
   366→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session: %w", err)
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session restored to: %s\n", sessionLogPath)
   370→	return nil
   371→}
   372→
   373→// runSessionResumeInteractive shows an interactive picker for sessions.
   374→func runSessionResumeInteractive() error {
   375→	sessions, err := strategy.ListSessions()
   376→	if err != nil {
   377→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list sessions: %w", err)
   378→	}
   379→
   380→	if len(sessions) == 0 {
   381→		return errors.New("no sessions found")
   382→	}
   383→
   384→	// Filter sessions to only those relevant to the current branch
   385→	sessions = filterSessionsForCurrentBranch(sessions)
   386→
   387→	if len(sessions) == 0 {
   388→		return errors.New("no sessions found for current branch")
   389→	}
   390→
   391→	// Sort sessions by most recent activity
   392→	sort.Slice(sessions, func(i, j int) bool {
   393→		iTime := sessions[i].StartTime
   394→		for _, cp := range sessions[i].Checkpoints {
   395→			if cp.Timestamp.After(iTime) {
   396→				iTime = cp.Timestamp
   397→			}
   398→		}
   399→		jTime := sessions[j].StartTime
   400→		for _, cp := range sessions[j].Checkpoints {
   401→			if cp.Timestamp.After(jTime) {
   402→				jTime = cp.Timestamp
   403→			}
   404→		}
   405→		if iTime.Equal(jTime) {
   406→			return sessions[i].ID > sessions[j].ID
   407→		}
   408→		return iTime.After(jTime)
   409→	})
   410→
   411→	// Build options for the picker
   412→	options := buildSessionOptions(sessions)
   413→
   414→	var selectedID string
   415→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   416→		huh.NewGroup(
   417→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   418→				Title("Select a session to resume").
   419→				Description("Choose a session to set as current and restore agent memory").
   420→				Options(options...).
   421→				Value(&selectedID),
   422→		),
   423→	)
   424→
   425→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   426→		if errors.Is(err, huh.ErrUserAborted) {
   427→			return fmt.Errorf("selection cancelled: %w", err)
   428→		}
   429→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get selection: %w", err)
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	if selectedID == sessionPickerCancelValue {
   433→		// User selected cancel
   434→		fmt.Println("Session resume cancelled.")
   435→		return nil
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	return runSessionResume(selectedID)
   439→}
   440→
   441→// filterSessionsForCurrentBranch filters sessions to only those linked to the current branch.
   442→// Finds Entire-Checkpoint trailers in branch commits.
   443→func filterSessionsForCurrentBranch(sessions []strategy.Session) []strategy.Session {
   444→	repo, err := openRepository()
   445→	if err != nil {
   446→		return sessions
   447→	}
   448→
   449→	head, err := repo.Head()
   450→	if err != nil {
   451→		return sessions
   452→	}
   453→
   454→	headHash := head.Hash()
   455→	matchingSessionIDs := make(map[string]bool)
   456→
   457→	// Find Entire-Checkpoint trailers in branch commits
   458→	checkpointToSession := make(map[string]string)
   459→	for _, sess := range sessions {
   460→		for _, cp := range sess.Checkpoints {
   461→			if cp.CheckpointID != "" {
   462→				checkpointToSession[cp.CheckpointID] = sess.ID
   463→			}
   464→		}
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: headHash})
   468→	if err != nil {
   469→		return sessions
   470→	}
   471→	// Get the main branch commit hash to determine branch-only commits
   472→	mainBranchHash := strategy.GetMainBranchHash(repo)
   473→
   474→	count := 0
   475→	_ = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort search
   476→		count++
   477→		if count > maxCommitsToScanForCheckpointsUntilMainBranch {
   478→			return errors.New("limit reached")
   479→		}
   480→		if mainBranchHash != plumbing.ZeroHash && c.Hash.String() == mainBranchHash.String() {
   481→			return nil
   482→		}
   483→
   484→		if checkpointID, found := paths.ParseCheckpointTrailer(c.Message); found {
   485→			if sessionID, ok := checkpointToSession[checkpointID]; ok {
   486→				matchingSessionIDs[sessionID] = true
   487→			}
   488→		}
   489→		return nil
   490→	})
   491→
   492→	// Filter sessions
   493→	filtered := make([]strategy.Session, 0, len(sessions))
   494→	for _, sess := range sessions {
   495→		if matchingSessionIDs[sess.ID] {
   496→			filtered = append(filtered, sess)
   497→		}
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	return filtered
   501→}
   502→
   503→// buildSessionOptions creates huh.Option items from sessions for the picker.
   504→func buildSessionOptions(sessions []strategy.Session) []huh.Option[string] {
   505→	options := make([]huh.Option[string], 0, len(sessions)+1)
   506→
   507→	for _, sess := range sessions {
   508→		// Format: session-id (N checkpoints) - description
   509→		displayID := sess.ID
   510→		if len(displayID) > 19 {
   511→			displayID = displayID[:19]
   512→		}
   513→
   514→		label := displayID
   515→		if len(sess.Checkpoints) > 0 {
   516→			label = fmt.Sprintf("%s (%d checkpoints)", displayID, len(sess.Checkpoints))
   517→		}
   518→		if sess.Description != "" && sess.Description != strategy.NoDescription {
   519→			desc := sess.Description
   520→			if len(desc) > 40 {
   521→				desc = desc[:37] + "..."
   522→			}
   523→			label = fmt.Sprintf("%s - %s", label, desc)
   524→		}
   525→
   526→		options = append(options, huh.NewOption(label, sess.ID))
   527→	}
   528→
   529→	// Add cancel option
   530→	options = append(options, huh.NewOption("Cancel", sessionPickerCancelValue))
   531→
   532→	return options
   533→}
   534→
   535→func runSessionCurrent() error {
   536→	currentSessionID, err := paths.ReadCurrentSession()
   537→	if err != nil {
   538→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read current session: %w", err)
   539→	}
   540→
   541→	if currentSessionID == "" {
   542→		fmt.Println("No current session set.")
   543→		return nil
   544→	}
   545→
   546→	strat := GetStrategy()
   547→
   548→	session, sessErr := strategy.GetSession(currentSessionID)
   549→	if sessErr != nil {
   550→		// Session ID is set but not found in strategy
   551→		fmt.Printf("Session: %s (not found in %s strategy)\n", currentSessionID, strat.Name())
   552→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Display session ID even when not found in strategy
   553→	}
   554→
   555→	fmt.Printf("Session:     %s\n", session.ID)
   556→	// Use session.Strategy if available, otherwise fall back to current strategy
   557→	displayStrategy := session.Strategy
   558→	if displayStrategy == "" {
   559→		displayStrategy = strat.Name()
   560→	}
   561→	fmt.Printf("Strategy:    %s\n", displayStrategy)
   562→	if session.Description != "" && session.Description != strategy.NoDescription {
   563→		fmt.Printf("Description: %s\n", session.Description)
   564→	}
   565→	if !session.StartTime.IsZero() {
   566→		fmt.Printf("Started:     %s\n", session.StartTime.Format("2006-01-02 15:04"))
   567→	}
   568→	if len(session.Checkpoints) > 0 {
   569→		fmt.Printf("Checkpoints: %d\n", len(session.Checkpoints))
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	return nil
   573→}
   574→

    31→func TestCopyMetadataDir_SkipsSymlinks(t *testing.T) {
    32→	// Create a temp directory for the test

    34→
    35→	// Initialize a git repository

    39→	}
    40→
    41→	// Create metadata directory structure

    43→	if err := os.MkdirAll(metadataDir, 0755); err != nil {
    44→		t.Fatalf("failed to create metadata dir: %v", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Create a regular file that should be included
    48→	regularFile := filepath.Join(metadataDir, "regular.txt")

    50→		t.Fatalf("failed to create regular file: %v", err)
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	// Create a sensitive file outside the metadata directory

    55→	if err := os.WriteFile(sensitiveFile, []byte("SECRET DATA"), 0644); err != nil {
    56→		t.Fatalf("failed to create sensitive file: %v", err)
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	// Create a symlink inside metadata directory pointing to the sensitive file

    62→		t.Fatalf("failed to create symlink: %v", err)
    63→	}

    65→	// Create GitStore and call copyMetadataDir

   186→// TestWriteTemporary_Deduplication verifies that WriteTemporary skips creating
   187→// a new commit when the tree hash matches the previous checkpoint.
   188→func TestWriteTemporary_Deduplication(t *testing.T) {
   189→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   190→
   191→	// Initialize a git repository with an initial commit
   192→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(tempDir, false)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	// Create worktree and make initial commit
   198→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   199→	if err != nil {
   200→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	readmeFile := filepath.Join(tempDir, "README.md")
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(readmeFile, []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   205→		t.Fatalf("failed to write README: %v", err)
   206→	}
   207→	if _, err := worktree.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   208→		t.Fatalf("failed to add README: %v", err)
   209→	}
   210→	initialCommit, err := worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   211→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
   212→	})
   213→	if err != nil {
   214→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	// Change to temp dir so paths.RepoRoot() works correctly
   218→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   219→
   220→	// Create a test file that will be included in checkpoints
   221→	testFile := filepath.Join(tempDir, "test.go")
   222→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package main\n"), 0o644); err != nil {
   223→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test file: %v", err)
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	// Create metadata directory
   227→	metadataDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".entire", "metadata", "test-session")
   228→	if err := os.MkdirAll(metadataDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   229→		t.Fatalf("failed to create metadata dir: %v", err)
   230→	}
   231→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(metadataDir, "full.jsonl"), []byte(`{"test": true}`), 0o644); err != nil {
   232→		t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	// Create checkpoint store
   236→	store := NewGitStore(repo)
   237→
   238→	// First checkpoint should be created
   239→	baseCommit := initialCommit.String()
   240→	result1, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), WriteTemporaryOptions{
   241→		SessionID:         "test-session",
   242→		BaseCommit:        baseCommit,
   243→		ModifiedFiles:     []string{"test.go"},
   244→		MetadataDir:       ".entire/metadata/test-session",
   245→		MetadataDirAbs:    metadataDir,
   246→		CommitMessage:     "Checkpoint 1",
   247→		AuthorName:        "Test",
   248→		AuthorEmail:       "test@test.com",
   249→		IsFirstCheckpoint: true,
   250→	})
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		t.Fatalf("WriteTemporary() first call error = %v", err)
   253→	}
   254→	if result1.Skipped {
   255→		t.Error("first checkpoint should not be skipped")
   256→	}
   257→	if result1.CommitHash == plumbing.ZeroHash {
   258→		t.Error("first checkpoint should have a commit hash")
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	// Second checkpoint with identical content should be skipped
   262→	result2, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), WriteTemporaryOptions{
   263→		SessionID:         "test-session",
   264→		BaseCommit:        baseCommit,
   265→		ModifiedFiles:     []string{"test.go"},
   266→		MetadataDir:       ".entire/metadata/test-session",
   267→		MetadataDirAbs:    metadataDir,
   268→		CommitMessage:     "Checkpoint 2",
   269→		AuthorName:        "Test",
   270→		AuthorEmail:       "test@test.com",
   271→		IsFirstCheckpoint: false,
   272→	})
   273→	if err != nil {
   274→		t.Fatalf("WriteTemporary() second call error = %v", err)
   275→	}
   276→	if !result2.Skipped {
   277→		t.Error("second checkpoint with identical content should be skipped")
   278→	}
   279→	if result2.CommitHash != result1.CommitHash {
   280→		t.Errorf("skipped checkpoint should return previous commit hash, got %s, want %s",
   281→			result2.CommitHash, result1.CommitHash)
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	// Modify the file and create another checkpoint - should NOT be skipped
   285→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n"), 0o644); err != nil {
   286→		t.Fatalf("failed to modify test file: %v", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	result3, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), WriteTemporaryOptions{

   291→		BaseCommit:        baseCommit,
   292→		ModifiedFiles:     []string{"test.go"},
   293→		MetadataDir:       ".entire/metadata/test-session",

   295→		CommitMessage:     "Checkpoint 3",

   299→	})

   302→	}
   303→	if result3.Skipped {

   307→		t.Error("third checkpoint should have a different commit hash than first")
