   311→// setupBranchTestRepo creates a test repository with an initial commit.
   312→func setupBranchTestRepo(t *testing.T) (*git.Repository, plumbing.Hash) {
   313→	t.Helper()
   314→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   315→
   316→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(tempDir, false)
   317→	if err != nil {
   318→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   322→	if err != nil {
   323→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	readmeFile := filepath.Join(tempDir, "README.md")
   327→	if err := os.WriteFile(readmeFile, []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   328→		t.Fatalf("failed to write README: %v", err)
   329→	}
   330→	if _, err := worktree.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   331→		t.Fatalf("failed to add README: %v", err)
   332→	}
   333→	commitHash, err := worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   334→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
   335→	})
   336→	if err != nil {
   337→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   338→	}
   339→
   340→	return repo, commitHash
   341→}
   342→
   343→// verifyBranchInMetadata reads and verifies the branch field in metadata.json.
   344→func verifyBranchInMetadata(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, checkpointID, expectedBranch string, shouldOmit bool) {
   345→	t.Helper()
   346→
   347→	metadataRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   348→	if err != nil {
   349→		t.Fatalf("failed to get metadata branch reference: %v", err)
   350→	}
   351→
   352→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(metadataRef.Hash())
   353→	if err != nil {
   354→		t.Fatalf("failed to get commit object: %v", err)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   358→	if err != nil {
   359→		t.Fatalf("failed to get tree: %v", err)
   360→	}
   361→
   362→	shardedPath := paths.CheckpointPath(checkpointID)
   363→	metadataPath := shardedPath + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName
   364→	metadataFile, err := tree.File(metadataPath)
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		t.Fatalf("failed to find metadata.json at %s: %v", metadataPath, err)
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	content, err := metadataFile.Contents()
   370→	if err != nil {
   371→		t.Fatalf("failed to read metadata.json: %v", err)
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	var metadata CommittedMetadata
   375→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &metadata); err != nil {
   376→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse metadata.json: %v", err)
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	if metadata.Branch != expectedBranch {
   380→		t.Errorf("metadata.Branch = %q, want %q", metadata.Branch, expectedBranch)
   381→	}
   382→
   383→	if shouldOmit && strings.Contains(content, `"branch"`) {
   384→		t.Errorf("metadata.json should not contain 'branch' field when empty (omitempty), got:\n%s", content)
   385→	}
   386→}
   387→
   388→// TestWriteCommitted_BranchField verifies that the Branch field is correctly
   389→// captured in metadata.json when on a branch, and is empty when in detached HEAD.
   390→func TestWriteCommitted_BranchField(t *testing.T) {
   391→	t.Run("on branch", func(t *testing.T) {
   392→		repo, commitHash := setupBranchTestRepo(t)
   393→
   394→		// Create a feature branch and switch to it
   395→		branchName := "feature/test-branch"
   396→		branchRef := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName)
   397→		ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(branchRef, commitHash)
   398→		if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   399→			t.Fatalf("failed to create branch: %v", err)
   400→		}
   401→
   402→		worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   403→		if err != nil {
   404→			t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   405→		}
   406→		if err := worktree.Checkout(&git.CheckoutOptions{Branch: branchRef}); err != nil {
   407→			t.Fatalf("failed to checkout branch: %v", err)
   408→		}
   409→
   410→		// Get current branch name
   411→		var currentBranch string
   412→		head, err := repo.Head()
   413→		if err == nil && head.Name().IsBranch() {
   414→			currentBranch = head.Name().Short()
   415→		}
   416→
   417→		// Write a committed checkpoint with branch information
   418→		checkpointID := "a1b2c3d4e5f6"
   419→		store := NewGitStore(repo)
   420→		err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), WriteCommittedOptions{
   421→			CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   422→			SessionID:    "test-session-123",
   423→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   424→			Branch:       currentBranch,
   425→			Transcript:   []byte("test transcript content"),
   426→			AuthorName:   "Test Author",
   427→			AuthorEmail:  "test@example.com",
   428→		})
   429→		if err != nil {
   430→			t.Fatalf("WriteCommitted() error = %v", err)
   431→		}
   432→
   433→		verifyBranchInMetadata(t, repo, checkpointID, branchName, false)
   434→	})
   435→
   436→	t.Run("detached HEAD", func(t *testing.T) {
   437→		repo, commitHash := setupBranchTestRepo(t)
   438→
   439→		// Checkout the commit directly (detached HEAD)
   440→		worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   441→		if err != nil {
   442→			t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   443→		}
   444→		if err := worktree.Checkout(&git.CheckoutOptions{Hash: commitHash}); err != nil {
   445→			t.Fatalf("failed to checkout commit: %v", err)
   446→		}
   447→
   448→		// Verify we're in detached HEAD
   449→		head, err := repo.Head()
   450→		if err != nil {
   451→			t.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   452→		}
   453→		if head.Name().IsBranch() {
   454→			t.Fatalf("expected detached HEAD, but on branch %s", head.Name().Short())
   455→		}
   456→
   457→		// Write a committed checkpoint (branch should be empty in detached HEAD)
   458→		checkpointID := "b2c3d4e5f6a7"
   459→		store := NewGitStore(repo)
   460→		err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), WriteCommittedOptions{
   461→			CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   462→			SessionID:    "test-session-456",
   463→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   464→			Branch:       "", // Empty when in detached HEAD
   465→			Transcript:   []byte("test transcript content"),
   466→			AuthorName:   "Test Author",
   467→			AuthorEmail:  "test@example.com",
   468→		})
   469→		if err != nil {
   470→			t.Fatalf("WriteCommitted() error = %v", err)
   471→		}
   472→
   473→		verifyBranchInMetadata(t, repo, checkpointID, "", true)
   474→	})
   475→}
   476→

   198→func TestReadWriteCurrentSession(t *testing.T) {
   199→	// Create temp directory to act as repo root
   200→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   201→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   202→
   203→	// Test reading non-existent file returns empty string (not error)
   204→	sessionID, err := ReadCurrentSession()
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() on non-existent file error = %v, want nil", err)
   207→	}
   208→	if sessionID != "" {
   209→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() on non-existent file = %q, want empty string", sessionID)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Test writing creates directory and file
   213→	testSessionID := "2025-12-01-8f76b0e8-b8f1-4a87-9186-848bdd83d62e"
   214→	err = WriteCurrentSession(testSessionID)
   215→	if err != nil {
   216→		t.Fatalf("WriteCurrentSession() error = %v", err)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	// Verify file exists
   220→	if _, err := os.Stat(CurrentSessionFile); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   221→		t.Error("WriteCurrentSession() did not create file")
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Test reading back the session ID
   225→	readSessionID, err := ReadCurrentSession()
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() error = %v, want nil", err)
   228→	}
   229→	if readSessionID != testSessionID {
   230→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() = %q, want %q", readSessionID, testSessionID)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Test overwriting existing session
   234→	newSessionID := "2025-12-02-abcd1234"
   235→	err = WriteCurrentSession(newSessionID)
   236→	if err != nil {
   237→		t.Errorf("WriteCurrentSession() overwrite error = %v", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	readSessionID, err = ReadCurrentSession()
   241→	if err != nil {
   242→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() after overwrite error = %v", err)
   243→	}
   244→	if readSessionID != newSessionID {
   245→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() after overwrite = %q, want %q", readSessionID, newSessionID)
   246→	}
   247→}

   378→		name      string
   379→		message   string
   380→		wantID    string
   381→		wantFound bool
   382→	}{
   383→		{
   384→			name:      "single session trailer",
   385→			message:   "Update logic\n\nEntire-Session: 2025-12-10-abc123def\n",
   386→			wantID:    "2025-12-10-abc123def",
   387→			wantFound: true,
   388→		},
   389→		{
   390→			name:      "no trailer",
   391→			message:   "Simple commit message",
   392→			wantID:    "",
   393→			wantFound: false,
   394→		},
   395→		{
   396→			name:      "trailer with extra spaces",
   397→			message:   "Message\n\nEntire-Session:   2025-12-10-xyz789   \n",
   398→			wantID:    "2025-12-10-xyz789",
   399→			wantFound: true,
   400→		},
   401→		{
   402→			name:      "multiple trailers returns first",
   403→			message:   "Merge\n\nEntire-Session: session-1\nEntire-Session: session-2\n",
   404→			wantID:    "session-1",
   405→			wantFound: true,
   406→		},
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	for _, tt := range tests {
   410→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   411→			gotID, gotFound := ParseSessionTrailer(tt.message)
   412→			if gotFound != tt.wantFound {
   413→				t.Errorf("ParseSessionTrailer() found = %v, want %v", gotFound, tt.wantFound)
   414→			}
   415→			if gotID != tt.wantID {
   416→				t.Errorf("ParseSessionTrailer() id = %v, want %v", gotID, tt.wantID)
   417→			}
   418→		})
   419→	}
   420→}
   421→
   422→func TestParseAllSessionTrailers(t *testing.T) {
   423→	tests := []struct {
   424→		name    string
   425→		message string
   426→		want    []string
   427→	}{
   428→		{
   429→			name:    "single session trailer",
   430→			message: "Update logic\n\nEntire-Session: 2025-12-10-abc123def\n",
   431→			want:    []string{"2025-12-10-abc123def"},
   432→		},
   433→		{
   434→			name:    "no trailer",
   435→			message: "Simple commit message",
   436→			want:    nil,
   437→		},
   438→		{
   439→			name:    "multiple session trailers",
   440→			message: "Merge commit\n\nEntire-Session: session-1\nEntire-Session: session-2\nEntire-Session: session-3\n",
   441→			want:    []string{"session-1", "session-2", "session-3"},
   442→		},
   443→		{
   444→			name:    "duplicate session IDs are deduplicated",
   445→			message: "Merge\n\nEntire-Session: session-1\nEntire-Session: session-2\nEntire-Session: session-1\n",
   446→			want:    []string{"session-1", "session-2"},
   447→		},
   448→		{
   449→			name:    "trailers with extra spaces",
   450→			message: "Message\n\nEntire-Session:   session-a   \nEntire-Session:  session-b \n",
   451→			want:    []string{"session-a", "session-b"},
   452→		},
   453→		{
   454→			name:    "mixed with other trailers",
   455→			message: "Merge\n\nEntire-Session: session-1\nEntire-Metadata: .entire/metadata/xyz\nEntire-Session: session-2\n",
   456→			want:    []string{"session-1", "session-2"},
   457→		},
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	for _, tt := range tests {
   461→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   462→			got := ParseAllSessionTrailers(tt.message)
   463→			if len(got) != len(tt.want) {
   464→				t.Errorf("ParseAllSessionTrailers() returned %d items, want %d", len(got), len(tt.want))
   465→				t.Errorf("got: %v, want: %v", got, tt.want)
   466→				return
   467→			}
   468→			for i, wantID := range tt.want {
   469→				if got[i] != wantID {
   470→					t.Errorf("ParseAllSessionTrailers()[%d] = %v, want %v", i, got[i], wantID)
   471→				}
   472→			}
   473→		})
   474→	}
   475→}

    51→	})

    53→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)

    56→	t.Chdir(dir)

    93→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},

    95→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	if err := s.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
    99→		t.Fatalf("SaveChanges() error = %v", err)
   100→	}

   258→func TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveTaskCheckpoint_CommitHasMetadataRef(t *testing.T) {

   359→func TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveTaskCheckpoint_TaskStartCreatesEmptyCommit(t *testing.T) {
   360→	// Setup temp git repo
   361→	dir := t.TempDir()
   362→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   363→	if err != nil {
   364→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   365→	}
   366→
   367→	// Create initial commit (needed for a valid repo state)
   368→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   369→	if err != nil {
   370→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   371→	}
   372→	readmeFile := filepath.Join(dir, "README.md")
   373→	if err := os.WriteFile(readmeFile, []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   374→		t.Fatalf("failed to write README: %v", err)
   375→	}
   376→	if _, err := worktree.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   377→		t.Fatalf("failed to add README: %v", err)
   378→	}
   379→	initialCommit, err := worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   380→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
   381→	})
   382→	if err != nil {
   383→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	t.Chdir(dir)
   387→
   388→	// Setup strategy
   389→	s := NewAutoCommitStrategy()
   390→	if err := s.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   391→		t.Fatalf("EnsureSetup() error = %v", err)
   392→	}
   393→
   394→	// Create a TaskStart checkpoint with NO file changes
   395→	ctx := TaskCheckpointContext{
   396→		SessionID:           "test-session-taskstart",
   397→		ToolUseID:           "toolu_taskstart123",
   398→		IsIncremental:       true,
   399→		IncrementalType:     IncrementalTypeTaskStart,
   400→		IncrementalSequence: 1,
   401→		SubagentType:        "dev",
   402→		TaskDescription:     "Implement feature",
   403→		// No file changes
   404→		ModifiedFiles: []string{},
   405→		NewFiles:      []string{},
   406→		DeletedFiles:  []string{},
   407→		AuthorName:    "Test",
   408→		AuthorEmail:   "test@test.com",
   409→	}
   410→
   411→	if err := s.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   412→		t.Fatalf("SaveTaskCheckpoint() error = %v", err)
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	// Verify a NEW commit was created (not just returning HEAD)
   416→	head, err := repo.Head()
   417→	if err != nil {
   418→		t.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	if head.Hash() == initialCommit {
   422→		t.Error("TaskStart should create a new commit even without file changes, but HEAD is still the initial commit")
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	// Verify the commit message contains the expected content
   426→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   427→	if err != nil {
   428→		t.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD commit: %v", err)
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	// TaskStart commits should have "Starting" in the message
   432→	expectedSubstring := "Starting 'dev' agent: Implement feature"
   433→	if !strings.Contains(commit.Message, expectedSubstring) {
   434→		t.Errorf("commit message should contain %q, got:\n%s", expectedSubstring, commit.Message)
   435→	}
   436→}

   438→func TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveTaskCheckpoint_NonTaskStartNoChangesAmendedForMetadata(t *testing.T) {
   439→	// Setup temp git repo
   440→	dir := t.TempDir()
   441→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   442→	if err != nil {
   443→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   444→	}
   445→
   446→	// Create initial commit
   447→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   448→	if err != nil {
   449→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   450→	}
   451→	readmeFile := filepath.Join(dir, "README.md")
   452→	if err := os.WriteFile(readmeFile, []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   453→		t.Fatalf("failed to write README: %v", err)
   454→	}
   455→	if _, err := worktree.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   456→		t.Fatalf("failed to add README: %v", err)
   457→	}
   458→	initialCommit, err := worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   459→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
   460→	})
   461→	if err != nil {
   462→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   463→	}
   464→
   465→	t.Chdir(dir)
   466→
   467→	// Setup strategy
   468→	s := NewAutoCommitStrategy()
   469→	if err := s.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   470→		t.Fatalf("EnsureSetup() error = %v", err)
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	// Create a regular incremental checkpoint (not TaskStart) with NO file changes
   474→	ctx := TaskCheckpointContext{
   475→		SessionID:           "test-session-nontaskstart",
   476→		ToolUseID:           "toolu_nontaskstart456",
   477→		IsIncremental:       true,
   478→		IncrementalType:     "TodoWrite", // NOT TaskStart
   479→		IncrementalSequence: 2,
   480→		TodoContent:         "Write some code",
   481→		// No file changes
   482→		ModifiedFiles: []string{},
   483→		NewFiles:      []string{},
   484→		DeletedFiles:  []string{},
   485→		AuthorName:    "Test",
   486→		AuthorEmail:   "test@test.com",
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	if err := s.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   490→		t.Fatalf("SaveTaskCheckpoint() error = %v", err)
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	// Get HEAD after the operation
   494→	head, err := repo.Head()
   495→	if err != nil {
   496→		t.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	// The auto-commit strategy amends the HEAD commit to add source ref trailer,
   500→	// so HEAD will be different from the initial commit even without file changes.
   501→	// However, the commit tree should be the same as the initial commit.
   502→	newCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   503→	if err != nil {
   504→		t.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD commit: %v", err)
   505→	}
   506→
   507→	oldCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(initialCommit)
   508→	if err != nil {
   509→		t.Fatalf("failed to get initial commit: %v", err)
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	// The tree hash should be the same (no file changes)
   513→	if newCommit.TreeHash != oldCommit.TreeHash {
   514→		t.Error("non-TaskStart checkpoint without file changes should have the same tree hash")
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Metadata should still be stored on entire/sessions branch
   518→	metadataBranch, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   519→	if err != nil {
   520→		t.Fatalf("failed to get entire/sessions branch: %v", err)
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	metadataCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(metadataBranch.Hash())
   524→	if err != nil {
   525→		t.Fatalf("failed to get metadata commit: %v", err)
   526→	}
   527→
   528→	// Verify metadata was committed to the branch
   529→	if !strings.Contains(metadataCommit.Message, paths.MetadataTaskTrailerKey) {
   530→		t.Error("metadata should still be committed to entire/sessions branch")
   531→	}
   532→}

   534→func TestAutoCommitStrategy_GetSessionContext(t *testing.T) {
   535→	// Setup temp git repo
   536→	dir := t.TempDir()
   537→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   538→	if err != nil {
   539→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Create initial commit
   543→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   544→	if err != nil {
   545→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   546→	}
   547→	readmeFile := filepath.Join(dir, "README.md")
   548→	if err := os.WriteFile(readmeFile, []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   549→		t.Fatalf("failed to write README: %v", err)
   550→	}
   551→	if _, err := worktree.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   552→		t.Fatalf("failed to add README: %v", err)
   553→	}
   554→	_, err = worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   555→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
   556→	})
   557→	if err != nil {
   558→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	t.Chdir(dir)
   562→
   563→	// Setup strategy
   564→	s := NewAutoCommitStrategy()
   565→	if err := s.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   566→		t.Fatalf("EnsureSetup() error = %v", err)
   567→	}
   568→
   569→	// Create a modified file
   570→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.go")
   571→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package main"), 0o644); err != nil {
   572→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test file: %v", err)
   573→	}
   574→
   575→	// Create metadata directory with session log and context.md
   576→	sessionID := "2025-12-10-test-session-context"
   577→	metadataDir := filepath.Join(dir, paths.EntireMetadataDir, sessionID)
   578→	if err := os.MkdirAll(metadataDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   579→		t.Fatalf("failed to create metadata dir: %v", err)
   580→	}
   581→	logFile := filepath.Join(metadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   582→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, []byte("test session log"), 0o644); err != nil {
   583→		t.Fatalf("failed to write log file: %v", err)
   584→	}
   585→	contextContent := "# Session Context\n\nThis is a test context.\n\n## Details\n\n- Item 1\n- Item 2"
   586→	contextFile := filepath.Join(metadataDir, paths.ContextFileName)
   587→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(contextContent), 0o644); err != nil {
   588→		t.Fatalf("failed to write context file: %v", err)
   589→	}
   590→
   591→	metadataDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(metadataDir)
   592→	if err != nil {
   593→		metadataDirAbs = metadataDir
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Save changes - this creates a checkpoint on entire/sessions
   597→	ctx := SaveContext{
   598→		CommitMessage:  "Test checkpoint",
   599→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   600→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   601→		NewFiles:       []string{"test.go"},
   602→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   603→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   604→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   605→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   606→	}
   607→	if err := s.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   608→		t.Fatalf("SaveChanges() error = %v", err)
   609→	}
   610→
   611→	// Now retrieve the context using GetSessionContext
   612→	result := s.GetSessionContext(sessionID)
   613→	if result == "" {
   614→		t.Error("GetSessionContext() returned empty string")
   615→	}
   616→	if result != contextContent {
   617→		t.Errorf("GetSessionContext() = %q, want %q", result, contextContent)
   618→	}
   619→}

   621→func TestAutoCommitStrategy_ListSessions_HasDescription(t *testing.T) {
   622→	// Setup temp git repo
   623→	dir := t.TempDir()
   624→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   625→	if err != nil {
   626→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	// Create initial commit
   630→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   631→	if err != nil {
   632→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   633→	}
   634→	readmeFile := filepath.Join(dir, "README.md")
   635→	if err := os.WriteFile(readmeFile, []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   636→		t.Fatalf("failed to write README: %v", err)
   637→	}
   638→	if _, err := worktree.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   639→		t.Fatalf("failed to add README: %v", err)
   640→	}
   641→	_, err = worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   642→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
   643→	})
   644→	if err != nil {
   645→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	t.Chdir(dir)
   649→
   650→	// Setup strategy
   651→	s := NewAutoCommitStrategy()
   652→	if err := s.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   653→		t.Fatalf("EnsureSetup() error = %v", err)
   654→	}
   655→
   656→	// Create a modified file
   657→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.go")
   658→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package main"), 0o644); err != nil {
   659→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test file: %v", err)
   660→	}
   661→
   662→	// Create metadata directory with session log and prompt.txt
   663→	sessionID := "2025-12-10-test-session-description"
   664→	metadataDir := filepath.Join(dir, paths.EntireMetadataDir, sessionID)
   665→	if err := os.MkdirAll(metadataDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   666→		t.Fatalf("failed to create metadata dir: %v", err)
   667→	}
   668→	logFile := filepath.Join(metadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   669→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, []byte("test session log"), 0o644); err != nil {
   670→		t.Fatalf("failed to write log file: %v", err)
   671→	}
   672→
   673→	// Write prompt.txt with description
   674→	expectedDescription := "Fix the authentication bug in login.go"
   675→	promptFile := filepath.Join(metadataDir, paths.PromptFileName)
   676→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(expectedDescription+"\n\nMore details here..."), 0o644); err != nil {
   677→		t.Fatalf("failed to write prompt file: %v", err)
   678→	}
   679→
   680→	metadataDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(metadataDir)
   681→	if err != nil {
   682→		metadataDirAbs = metadataDir
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	// Save changes - this creates a checkpoint on entire/sessions
   686→	ctx := SaveContext{
   687→		CommitMessage:  "Test checkpoint",
   688→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   689→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   690→		NewFiles:       []string{"test.go"},
   691→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   692→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   693→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   694→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   695→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   696→	}
   697→	if err := s.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   698→		t.Fatalf("SaveChanges() error = %v", err)
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	sessions, err := ListSessions()
   702→	if err != nil {
   703→		t.Fatalf("ListSessions() error = %v", err)
   704→	}
   705→
   706→	if len(sessions) == 0 {
   707→		t.Fatal("ListSessions() returned no sessions")
   708→	}
   709→
   710→	// Find our session
   711→	var found *Session
   712→	for i := range sessions {
   713→		if sessions[i].ID == sessionID {
   714→			found = &sessions[i]
   715→			break
   716→		}
   717→	}
   718→
   719→	if found == nil {
   720→		t.Fatalf("Session %q not found in ListSessions() result", sessionID)
   721→	}
   722→
   723→	// Verify description is populated (not "No description")
   724→	if found.Description == NoDescription {
   725→		t.Errorf("ListSessions() returned session with Description = %q, want %q", found.Description, expectedDescription)
   726→	}
   727→	if found.Description != expectedDescription {
   728→		t.Errorf("ListSessions() returned session with Description = %q, want %q", found.Description, expectedDescription)
   729→	}
   730→}

   780→	sessionID := "2025-12-22-test-session-init"
   781→	if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, "Claude Code", ""); err != nil {
   782→		t.Fatalf("InitializeSession() error = %v", err)
   783→	}
   784→
   785→	// Verify session state was created
   786→	state, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   787→	if err != nil {

   792→	}

   795→		t.Errorf("SessionID = %q, want %q", state.SessionID, sessionID)
   796→	}
   797→	if state.CheckpointCount != 0 {
   798→		t.Errorf("CheckpointCount = %d, want 0", state.CheckpointCount)
   799→	}
   800→	if state.CondensedTranscriptLines != 0 {
   801→		t.Errorf("CondensedTranscriptLines = %d, want 0", state.CondensedTranscriptLines)
   802→	}
   803→}

   805→func TestAutoCommitStrategy_GetCheckpointLog_ReadsFullJsonl(t *testing.T) {
   806→	// Setup temp git repo
   807→	dir := t.TempDir()
   808→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   809→	if err != nil {
   810→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   811→	}
   812→
   813→	// Create initial commit
   814→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   815→	if err != nil {
   816→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   817→	}
   818→	readmeFile := filepath.Join(dir, "README.md")
   819→	if err := os.WriteFile(readmeFile, []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   820→		t.Fatalf("failed to write README: %v", err)
   821→	}
   822→	if _, err := worktree.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   823→		t.Fatalf("failed to add README: %v", err)
   824→	}
   825→	_, err = worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   826→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
   827→	})
   828→	if err != nil {
   829→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   830→	}
   831→
   832→	t.Chdir(dir)
   833→
   834→	// Setup strategy
   835→	s := NewAutoCommitStrategy()
   836→	if err := s.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   837→		t.Fatalf("EnsureSetup() error = %v", err)
   838→	}
   839→
   840→	// Create a file for the task checkpoint
   841→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "task_output.txt")
   842→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("task result"), 0o644); err != nil {
   843→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test file: %v", err)
   844→	}
   845→
   846→	// Create transcript file with expected content
   847→	transcriptDir := t.TempDir()
   848→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(transcriptDir, "session.jsonl")
   849→	expectedContent := `{"type":"assistant","content":"test response"}`
   850→	if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(expectedContent), 0o644); err != nil {
   851→		t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   852→	}
   853→
   854→	sessionID := "2025-12-12-test-checkpoint-jsonl"
   855→
   856→	// Call SaveTaskCheckpoint (final, not incremental - this includes full.jsonl)
   857→	ctx := TaskCheckpointContext{
   858→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   859→		ToolUseID:      "toolu_jsonl_test",
   860→		CheckpointUUID: "checkpoint-uuid-jsonl",
   861→		AgentID:        "agent-jsonl",
   862→		TranscriptPath: transcriptPath,
   863→		NewFiles:       []string{"task_output.txt"},
   864→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   865→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   866→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   867→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   868→	}
   869→
   870→	if err := s.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   871→		t.Fatalf("SaveTaskCheckpoint() error = %v", err)
   872→	}
   873→
   874→	sessions, err := ListSessions()
   875→	if err != nil {
   876→		t.Fatalf("ListSessions() error = %v", err)
   877→	}
   878→
   879→	var session *Session
   880→	for i := range sessions {
   881→		if sessions[i].ID == sessionID {
   882→			session = &sessions[i]
   883→			break
   884→		}
   885→	}
   886→	if session == nil {
   887→		t.Fatalf("Session %q not found", sessionID)
   888→	}
   889→	if len(session.Checkpoints) == 0 {
   890→		t.Fatal("No checkpoints found for session")
   891→	}
   892→
   893→	// Get checkpoint log - should read full.jsonl
   894→	checkpoint := session.Checkpoints[0]
   895→	content, err := s.GetCheckpointLog(checkpoint)
   896→	if err != nil {
   897→		t.Fatalf("GetCheckpointLog() error = %v", err)
   898→	}
   899→
   900→	if string(content) != expectedContent {
   901→		t.Errorf("GetCheckpointLog() content = %q, want %q", string(content), expectedContent)
   902→	}
   903→}

   905→// TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveChanges_FilesAlreadyCommitted verifies that SaveChanges
   906→// skips creating metadata when files are listed but already committed by the user.
   907→// This handles the case where git.ErrEmptyCommit occurs during commit.
   908→func TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveChanges_FilesAlreadyCommitted(t *testing.T) {
   909→	// Setup temp git repo
   910→	dir := t.TempDir()
   911→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   912→	if err != nil {
   913→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   914→	}
   915→
   916→	// Create initial commit
   917→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   918→	if err != nil {
   919→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   920→	}
   921→	readmeFile := filepath.Join(dir, "README.md")
   922→	if err := os.WriteFile(readmeFile, []byte("# Test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   923→		t.Fatalf("failed to write README: %v", err)
   924→	}
   925→	if _, err := worktree.Add("README.md"); err != nil {
   926→		t.Fatalf("failed to add README: %v", err)
   927→	}
   928→	_, err = worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
   929→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
   930→	})
   931→	if err != nil {
   932→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   933→	}
   934→
   935→	t.Chdir(dir)
   936→
   937→	// Setup strategy
   938→	s := NewAutoCommitStrategy()
   939→	if err := s.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   940→		t.Fatalf("EnsureSetup() error = %v", err)
   941→	}
   942→
   943→	// Create a test file and commit it manually (simulating user committing before hook runs)
   944→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.go")
   945→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("package main"), 0o644); err != nil {
   946→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test file: %v", err)
   947→	}
   948→	if _, err := worktree.Add("test.go"); err != nil {
   949→		t.Fatalf("failed to add test file: %v", err)
   950→	}
   951→	userCommit, err := worktree.Commit("User committed the file first", &git.CommitOptions{
   952→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "User", Email: "user@test.com"},
   953→	})
   954→	if err != nil {
   955→		t.Fatalf("failed to commit test file: %v", err)
   956→	}
   957→
   958→	// Get count of commits on entire/sessions before the call
   959→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   960→	if err != nil {
   961→		t.Fatalf("entire/sessions branch not found: %v", err)
   962→	}
   963→	sessionsCommitBefore := sessionsRef.Hash()
   964→
   965→	// Create metadata directory

  1019→// TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveChanges_NoChangesSkipped verifies that SaveChanges
  1020→// skips creating metadata when there are no code changes to commit.
  1021→// This ensures 1:1 mapping between code commits and metadata commits.
  1022→func TestAutoCommitStrategy_SaveChanges_NoChangesSkipped(t *testing.T) {
  1023→	// Setup temp git repo

  1038→	}

  1042→	initialCommit, err := worktree.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
  1043→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
  1044→	})

  1047→	}
  1048→

  1050→

  1053→	if err := s.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {

  1055→	}

  1057→	// Get count of commits on entire/sessions before the call
  1058→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
  1059→	if err != nil {
  1060→		t.Fatalf("entire/sessions branch not found: %v", err)
  1061→	}

  1064→	// Create metadata directory (without any file changes to commit)
  1065→	sessionID := "2025-12-22-no-changes-test"

  1078→	}

  1080→	// Call SaveChanges with NO file changes (empty lists)

  1086→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{}, // Empty - no changes
  1087→		DeletedFiles:   []string{}, // Empty - no changes
  1088→		AuthorName:     "Test",
  1089→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
  1090→	}

  1092→	// SaveChanges should succeed without error (skip is not an error)

  1095→	}

  1104→	}

  1106→	// Verify entire/sessions branch has no new commits (metadata not created)
  1107→	sessionsRefAfter, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)

   198→func TestReadWriteCurrentSession(t *testing.T) {
   199→	// Create temp directory to act as repo root
   200→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   201→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   202→
   203→	// Test reading non-existent file returns empty string (not error)
   204→	sessionID, err := ReadCurrentSession()
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() on non-existent file error = %v, want nil", err)
   207→	}
   208→	if sessionID != "" {
   209→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() on non-existent file = %q, want empty string", sessionID)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Test writing creates directory and file
   213→	testSessionID := "2025-12-01-8f76b0e8-b8f1-4a87-9186-848bdd83d62e"
   214→	err = WriteCurrentSession(testSessionID)
   215→	if err != nil {
   216→		t.Fatalf("WriteCurrentSession() error = %v", err)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	// Verify file exists
   220→	if _, err := os.Stat(CurrentSessionFile); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   221→		t.Error("WriteCurrentSession() did not create file")
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Test reading back the session ID
   225→	readSessionID, err := ReadCurrentSession()
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() error = %v, want nil", err)
   228→	}
   229→	if readSessionID != testSessionID {
   230→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() = %q, want %q", readSessionID, testSessionID)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Test overwriting existing session
   234→	newSessionID := "2025-12-02-abcd1234"
   235→	err = WriteCurrentSession(newSessionID)
   236→	if err != nil {
   237→		t.Errorf("WriteCurrentSession() overwrite error = %v", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	readSessionID, err = ReadCurrentSession()
   241→	if err != nil {
   242→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() after overwrite error = %v", err)
   243→	}
   244→	if readSessionID != newSessionID {
   245→		t.Errorf("ReadCurrentSession() after overwrite = %q, want %q", readSessionID, newSessionID)
   246→	}
   247→}

   316→func TestEntireSessionID(t *testing.T) {
   317→	claudeSessionID := "8f76b0e8-b8f1-4a87-9186-848bdd83d62e"
   318→
   319→	result := EntireSessionID(claudeSessionID)
   320→
   321→	// Should match format: YYYY-MM-DD-<claude-session-id>
   322→	pattern := `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}-` + regexp.QuoteMeta(claudeSessionID) + `$`
   323→	matched, err := regexp.MatchString(pattern, result)
   324→	if err != nil {
   325→		t.Fatalf("regex error: %v", err)
   326→	}
   327→	if !matched {
   328→		t.Errorf("EntireSessionID() = %q, want format YYYY-MM-DD-%s", result, claudeSessionID)
   329→	}
   330→}

   332→func TestEntireSessionID_PreservesInput(t *testing.T) {
   333→	tests := []struct {
   334→		name            string
   335→		claudeSessionID string
   336→	}{
   337→		{"simple uuid", "abc123"},
   338→		{"full uuid", "8f76b0e8-b8f1-4a87-9186-848bdd83d62e"},
   339→		{"with special chars", "test-session_123"},
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	for _, tt := range tests {
   343→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   344→			result := EntireSessionID(tt.claudeSessionID)
   345→
   346→			// Should end with the original Claude session ID
   347→			suffix := "-" + tt.claudeSessionID
   348→			if len(result) < len(suffix) || result[len(result)-len(suffix):] != suffix {
   349→				t.Errorf("EntireSessionID(%q) = %q, should end with %q", tt.claudeSessionID, result, suffix)
   350→			}
   351→
   352→			// Should start with date prefix (11 chars: YYYY-MM-DD-)
   353→			if len(result) < 11 {
   354→				t.Errorf("EntireSessionID(%q) = %q, too short for date prefix", tt.claudeSessionID, result)
   355→			}
   356→		})
   357→	}
   358→}

   110→// IsEmpty returns true if the checkpoint ID is empty or unset.
   111→func (id CheckpointID) IsEmpty() bool {
   112→	return id == EmptyCheckpointID
   113→}
   114→
   115→// Path returns the sharded path for this checkpoint ID on entire/sessions.
   116→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
   117→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
   118→func (id CheckpointID) Path() string {
   119→	if len(id) < 3 {
   120→		return string(id)
   121→	}
   122→	return string(id[:2]) + "/" + string(id[2:])
   123→}
   124→
   125→// ValidateCheckpointID validates that a string is a valid checkpoint ID format.
   126→// Returns an error if invalid, nil if valid.
   127→func ValidateCheckpointID(id string) error {
   128→	if !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(id) {
   129→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", id)
   130→	}
   131→	return nil
   132→}
   133→
   134→// CheckpointPath returns the sharded path for a checkpoint ID string.
   135→// This is a convenience wrapper around CheckpointID.Path() for use with string IDs.
   136→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
   137→func CheckpointPath(checkpointID string) string {
   138→	return CheckpointID(checkpointID).Path()
   139→}
   140→
   141→// ParseStrategy extracts strategy from commit message.
   142→// Returns the strategy name and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   143→func ParseStrategy(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   144→	matches := strategyTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   145→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   146→		return NormalizeStrategyName(strings.TrimSpace(matches[1])), true
   147→	}
   148→	return "", false
   149→}

   217→// ParseAllSessions extracts all session IDs from a commit message.
   218→// Returns a slice of session IDs (may be empty if none found).
   219→// This is useful for commits that may have multiple Entire-Session trailers.
   220→func ParseAllSessions(commitMessage string) []string {
   221→	matches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindAllStringSubmatch(commitMessage, -1)
   222→	if len(matches) == 0 {
   223→		return nil
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	sessionIDs := make([]string, 0, len(matches))
   227→	for _, match := range matches {
   228→		if len(match) > 1 {
   229→			sessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, strings.TrimSpace(match[1]))
   230→		}
   231→	}
   232→	return sessionIDs
   233→}
   234→
   235→// NormalizeStrategyName maps legacy strategy names to current names.
   236→// These are the strategy names used during initial development and are kept for backwards
   237→// compatibility.
   238→func NormalizeStrategyName(name string) string {
   239→	switch name {
   240→	case "dual":
   241→		return "auto-commit"
   242→	case "shadow":
   243→		return "manual-commit"
   244→	default:
   245→		return name
   246→	}
   247→}

  "old_string": "// ParseAllSessions extracts all session IDs from a commit message.\n// Returns a slice of session IDs (may be empty if none found).\n// This is useful for commits that may have multiple Entire-Session trailers.\nfunc ParseAllSessions(commitMessage string) []string {\n\tmatches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindAllStringSubmatch(commitMessage, -1)\n\tif len(matches) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tsessionIDs := make([]string, 0, len(matches))\n\tfor _, match := range matches {\n\t\tif len(match) > 1 {\n\t\t\tsessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, strings.TrimSpace(match[1]))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn sessionIDs\n}",
  "new_string": "// ParseAllSessions extracts all session IDs from a commit message.\n// Returns a slice of session IDs (may be empty if none found).\n// Duplicate session IDs are deduplicated while preserving order.\n// This is useful for commits that may have multiple Entire-Session trailers.\nfunc ParseAllSessions(commitMessage string) []string {\n\tmatches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindAllStringSubmatch(commitMessage, -1)\n\tif len(matches) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tsessionIDs := make([]string, 0, len(matches))\n\tfor _, match := range matches {\n\t\tif len(match) > 1 {\n\t\t\tid := strings.TrimSpace(match[1])\n\t\t\tif !seen[id] {\n\t\t\t\tseen[id] = true\n\t\t\t\tsessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, id)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn sessionIDs\n}"
}

The trailers package refactoring is complete. Here's a summary of what was done:

**Created new `trailers` package** (`cmd/entire/cli/trailers/trailers.go`):
- `CheckpointID` type with methods (`String()`, `IsEmpty()`, `Path()`)
- All trailer key constants (`MetadataTrailerKey`, `SessionTrailerKey`, `CheckpointTrailerKey`, etc.)
- Parse functions (`ParseCheckpoint`, `ParseSession`, `ParseMetadata`, etc.) - `ParseCheckpoint` now returns `CheckpointID` type
- Format functions (`FormatMetadata`, `FormatTaskMetadata`, etc.)
- Helper functions (`GenerateCheckpointID`, `ValidateCheckpointID`, `CheckpointPath`)

**Updated packages** to use `trailers` instead of `paths` for trailer operations:
- `checkpoint/*`
- `strategy/*` 
- `cli/explain.go`, `cli/resume.go`, `cli/rewind.go`, `cli/session.go`
- All associated test files

**Key type changes:**
- `trailers.ParseCheckpoint()` returns `(CheckpointID, bool)` instead of `(string, bool)`
- Callers use `.String()` for string conversion and `.IsEmpty()` instead of `== ""`

The one remaining task is "Update struct fields to use CheckpointID type" which would provide stronger type safety throughout the codebase. This is a larger change that can be done incrementally if desired.

> DEVELOPER

hmm, some of the changes doesn't look / feel good now either, like basePath := trailers.CheckpointPath(opts.CheckpointID) + "/"

> AGENT

You're right, that does look awkward. `CheckpointPath` is really a path/storage concern, not a trailer concern. The `trailers` package should focus on commit message trailer parsing/formatting.

A cleaner organization might be:

**trailers package** - commit message operations only:
- Trailer key constants
- `Parse*` functions (extract values from commit messages)
- `Format*` functions (create commit messages with trailers)

**paths package** - file/directory paths:
- `CheckpointPath()` - constructs the sharded storage path
- Path constants

**For `CheckpointID` type**, a few options:

1. **Keep in trailers** but move `CheckpointPath()` back to paths - paths would call `trailers.CheckpointID(id).Path()` or just do the sharding itself

2. **Move `CheckpointID` to paths** - since its main purpose is path construction, and paths already has validation functions

3. **Create a dedicated `id` or `checkpoint/id` package** - if we want CheckpointID to be truly independent

What feels right to you? My instinct is option 2 - move `CheckpointID` type and `CheckpointPath` to `paths`, since the ID's primary purpose is to construct storage paths. The trailers package would just return strings from parsing (or import the type from paths).

> DEVELOPER

can paths just import checkpoint/id package (without all of checkpoint package)?

> AGENT

Yes, that would work well. We could create a small `checkpoint/id` subpackage:

```
checkpoint/id/          <- leaf package, no dependencies
├── id.go               <- CheckpointID type, Path(), validation, generation

paths/                  <- imports checkpoint/id
├── paths.go            <- can use id.CheckpointID, expose CheckpointPath()

trailers/               <- imports checkpoint/id (optional)
├── trailers.go         <- ParseCheckpoint returns id.CheckpointID

checkpoint/             <- imports paths, trailers, checkpoint/id
├── committed.go
├── temporary.go
```

The `checkpoint/id` package would be a leaf with no dependencies, so no cycles.

  "content": "// Package id provides the CheckpointID type for identifying checkpoints.\n// This is a separate package to avoid import cycles between paths, trailers, and checkpoint.\npackage id\n\nimport (\n\t\"crypto/rand\"\n\t\"encoding/hex\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n)\n\n// CheckpointID is a 12-character hex identifier for checkpoints.\n// It's used to link code commits to metadata on the entire/sessions branch.\ntype CheckpointID string\n\n// EmptyCheckpointID represents an unset or invalid checkpoint ID.\nconst EmptyCheckpointID CheckpointID = \"\"\n\n// checkpointIDRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.\nvar checkpointIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{12}$`)\n\n// NewCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, validating its format.\n// Returns an error if the string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.\nfunc NewCheckpointID(s string) (CheckpointID, error) {\n\tif err := Validate(s); err != nil {\n\t\treturn EmptyCheckpointID, err\n\t}\n\treturn CheckpointID(s), nil\n}\n\n// MustCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, panicking if invalid.\n// Use only when the ID is known to be valid (e.g., from trusted sources).\nfunc MustCheckpointID(s string) CheckpointID {\n\tid, err := NewCheckpointID(s)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tpanic(err)\n\t}\n\treturn id\n}\n\n// Generate creates a new random 12-character hex checkpoint ID.\nfunc Generate() CheckpointID {\n\tbytes := make([]byte, 6) // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars\n\tif _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {\n\t\tpanic(fmt.Sprintf(\"failed to generate random checkpoint ID: %v\", err))\n\t}\n\treturn CheckpointID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes))\n}\n\n// Validate checks if a string is a valid checkpoint ID format.\n// Returns an error if invalid, nil if valid.\nfunc Validate(s string) error {\n\tif !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(s) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters\", s)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// String returns the checkpoint ID as a string.\nfunc (id CheckpointID) String() string {\n\treturn string(id)\n}\n\n// IsEmpty returns true if the checkpoint ID is empty or unset.\nfunc (id CheckpointID) IsEmpty() bool {\n\treturn id == EmptyCheckpointID\n}\n\n// Path returns the sharded path for this checkpoint ID on entire/sessions.\n// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.\n// Example: \"a3b2c4d5e6f7\" -> \"a3/b2c4d5e6f7\"\nfunc (id CheckpointID) Path() string {\n\tif len(id) < 3 {\n\t\treturn string(id)\n\t}\n\treturn string(id[:2]) + \"/\" + string(id[2:])\n}\n"
}

     1→// Package trailers provides parsing and formatting for Entire commit message trailers.

    18→
    19→	// MetadataTaskTrailerKey points to the task metadata directory for subagent checkpoints.
    20→	MetadataTaskTrailerKey = "Entire-Metadata-Task"
    21→
    22→	// StrategyTrailerKey indicates which strategy created the commit.
    23→	StrategyTrailerKey = "Entire-Strategy"
    24→
    25→	// BaseCommitTrailerKey links shadow commits to their base code commit.
    26→	BaseCommitTrailerKey = "Base-Commit"
    27→
    28→	// SessionTrailerKey identifies which session created a commit.
    29→	SessionTrailerKey = "Entire-Session"
    30→
    31→	// CondensationTrailerKey identifies the condensation ID for a commit (legacy).
    32→	CondensationTrailerKey = "Entire-Condensation"
    33→
    34→	// SourceRefTrailerKey links code commits to their metadata on a shadow/metadata branch.
    35→	// Format: "<branch>@<commit-hash>" e.g. "entire/metadata@abc123def456"
    36→	SourceRefTrailerKey = "Entire-Source-Ref"
    37→
    38→	// CheckpointTrailerKey links commits to their checkpoint metadata on entire/sessions.
    39→	// Format: 12 hex characters e.g. "a3b2c4d5e6f7"
    40→	// This trailer survives git amend and rebase operations.
    41→	CheckpointTrailerKey = "Entire-Checkpoint"
    42→
    43→	// EphemeralBranchTrailerKey identifies the shadow branch that a checkpoint originated from.
    44→	// Used in manual-commit strategy checkpoint commits on entire/sessions branch.
    45→	// Format: full branch name e.g. "entire/2b4c177"
    46→	EphemeralBranchTrailerKey = "Ephemeral-branch"
    47→
    48→	// AgentTrailerKey identifies the agent that created a checkpoint.

    50→	AgentTrailerKey = "Entire-Agent"

   147→	}

   157→	}

   174→	matches := baseCommitTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   175→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   176→		return matches[1], true
   177→	}
   178→	return "", false
   179→}
   180→
   181→// ParseCondensation extracts the condensation ID from a commit message.
   182→// Returns the condensation ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   183→func ParseCondensation(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   184→	matches := condensationTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   185→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   186→		return strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true
   187→	}
   188→	return "", false
   189→}
   190→
   191→// ParseSession extracts the session ID from a commit message.
   192→// Returns the session ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   193→// Note: If multiple Entire-Session trailers exist, this returns only the first one.
   194→// Use ParseAllSessions to get all session IDs.
   195→func ParseSession(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {
   196→	matches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   197→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   198→		return strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true
   199→	}
   200→	return "", false
   201→}
   202→
   203→// ParseCheckpoint extracts the checkpoint ID from a commit message.
   204→// Returns the CheckpointID and true if found, EmptyCheckpointID and false otherwise.
   205→func ParseCheckpoint(commitMessage string) (CheckpointID, bool) {
   206→	matches := checkpointTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)
   207→	if len(matches) > 1 {
   208→		idStr := strings.TrimSpace(matches[1])
   209→		// Validate it's a proper checkpoint ID
   210→		if id, err := NewCheckpointID(idStr); err == nil {
   211→			return id, true
   212→		}
   213→	}
   214→	return EmptyCheckpointID, false
   215→}
   216→
   217→// ParseAllSessions extracts all session IDs from a commit message.
   218→// Returns a slice of session IDs (may be empty if none found).
   219→// Duplicate session IDs are deduplicated while preserving order.
   220→// This is useful for commits that may have multiple Entire-Session trailers.
   221→func ParseAllSessions(commitMessage string) []string {
   222→	matches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindAllStringSubmatch(commitMessage, -1)
   223→	if len(matches) == 0 {
   224→		return nil
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   228→	sessionIDs := make([]string, 0, len(matches))
   229→	for _, match := range matches {
   230→		if len(match) > 1 {
   231→			id := strings.TrimSpace(match[1])
   232→			if !seen[id] {
   233→				seen[id] = true
   234→				sessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, id)
   235→			}
   236→		}
   237→	}
   238→	return sessionIDs
   239→}

   241→// NormalizeStrategyName maps legacy strategy names to current names.
   242→// These are the strategy names used during initial development and are kept for backwards
   243→// compatibility.
   244→func NormalizeStrategyName(name string) string {
   245→	switch name {
   246→	case "dual":
   247→		return "auto-commit"
   248→	case "shadow":
   249→		return "manual-commit"
   250→	default:
   251→		return name
   252→	}
   253→}

   255→// FormatStrategy creates a commit message with just the strategy trailer.
   256→func FormatStrategy(message, strategy string) string {
   257→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\n%s: %s\n", message, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)
   258→}
   259→
   260→// FormatTaskMetadata creates a commit message with task metadata trailer.
   261→func FormatTaskMetadata(message, taskMetadataDir string) string {
   262→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\n%s: %s\n", message, MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataDir)
   263→}
   264→
   265→// FormatTaskMetadataWithStrategy creates a commit message with task metadata and strategy trailers.
   266→func FormatTaskMetadataWithStrategy(message, taskMetadataDir, strategy string) string {
   267→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\n%s: %s\n%s: %s\n", message, MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataDir, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)
   268→}
   269→
   270→// FormatSourceRef creates a formatted source ref string for the trailer.
   271→// Format: "<branch>@<commit-hash-prefix>" (hash truncated to 12 chars)
   272→func FormatSourceRef(branch, commitHash string) string {
   273→	shortHash := commitHash
   274→	if len(shortHash) > 12 {
   275→		shortHash = shortHash[:12]
   276→	}
   277→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s@%s", branch, shortHash)
   278→}
   279→
   280→// FormatMetadata creates a commit message with metadata trailer.
   281→func FormatMetadata(message, metadataDir string) string {
   282→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\n%s: %s\n", message, MetadataTrailerKey, metadataDir)
   283→}
   284→
   285→// FormatMetadataWithStrategy creates a commit message with metadata and strategy trailers.
   286→func FormatMetadataWithStrategy(message, metadataDir, strategy string) string {
   287→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\n%s: %s\n%s: %s\n", message, MetadataTrailerKey, metadataDir, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)
   288→}
   289→
   290→// FormatShadowCommit creates a commit message for manual-commit strategy checkpoints.
   291→// Includes Entire-Metadata, Entire-Session, and Entire-Strategy trailers.
   292→func FormatShadowCommit(message, metadataDir, sessionID string) string {
   293→	var sb strings.Builder
   294→	sb.WriteString(message)
   295→	sb.WriteString("\n\n")
   296→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", MetadataTrailerKey, metadataDir))
   297→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", SessionTrailerKey, sessionID))
   298→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", StrategyTrailerKey, "manual-commit"))
   299→	return sb.String()
   300→}
   301→
   302→// FormatShadowTaskCommit creates a commit message for manual-commit task checkpoints.
   303→// Includes Entire-Metadata-Task, Entire-Session, and Entire-Strategy trailers.
   304→func FormatShadowTaskCommit(message, taskMetadataDir, sessionID string) string {
   305→	var sb strings.Builder
   306→	sb.WriteString(message)
   307→	sb.WriteString("\n\n")
   308→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataDir))
   309→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", SessionTrailerKey, sessionID))
   310→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", StrategyTrailerKey, "manual-commit"))
   311→	return sb.String()
   312→}

if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseTaskMetadata(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := taskMetadataTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseBaseCommit extracts the base commit SHA from a commit message.\n// Returns the full SHA and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseBaseCommit(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := baseCommitTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn matches[1], true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseCondensation extracts the condensation ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the condensation ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseCondensation(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := condensationTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseSession extracts the session ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the session ID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.\n// Note: If multiple Entire-Session trailers exist, this returns only the first one.\n// Use ParseAllSessions to get all session IDs.\nfunc ParseSession(commitMessage string) (string, bool) {\n\tmatches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\treturn strings.TrimSpace(matches[1]), true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\n// ParseCheckpoint extracts the checkpoint ID from a commit message.\n// Returns the CheckpointID and true if found, empty ID and false otherwise.\nfunc ParseCheckpoint(commitMessage string) (id.CheckpointID, bool) {\n\tmatches := checkpointTrailerRegex.FindStringSubmatch(commitMessage)\n\tif len(matches) > 1 {\n\t\tidStr := strings.TrimSpace(matches[1])\n\t\t// Validate it's a proper checkpoint ID\n\t\tif cpID, err := id.NewCheckpointID(idStr); err == nil {\n\t\t\treturn cpID, true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn id.EmptyCheckpointID, false\n}\n\n// ParseAllSessions extracts all session IDs from a commit message.\n// Returns a slice of session IDs (may be empty if none found).\n// Duplicate session IDs are deduplicated while preserving order.\n// This is useful for commits that may have multiple Entire-Session trailers.\nfunc ParseAllSessions(commitMessage string) []string {\n\tmatches := sessionTrailerRegex.FindAllStringSubmatch(commitMessage, -1)\n\tif len(matches) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tsessionIDs := make([]string, 0, len(matches))\n\tfor _, match := range matches {\n\t\tif len(match) > 1 {\n\t\t\tsessionID := strings.TrimSpace(match[1])\n\t\t\tif !seen[sessionID] {\n\t\t\t\tseen[sessionID] = true\n\t\t\t\tsessionIDs = append(sessionIDs, sessionID)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn sessionIDs\n}\n\n// NormalizeStrategyName maps legacy strategy names to current names.\n// These are the strategy names used during initial development and are kept for backwards\n// compatibility.\nfunc NormalizeStrategyName(name string) string {\n\tswitch name {\n\tcase \"dual\":\n\t\treturn \"auto-commit\"\n\tcase \"shadow\":\n\t\treturn \"manual-commit\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn name\n\t}\n}\n\n// FormatStrategy creates a commit message with just the strategy trailer.\nfunc FormatStrategy(message, strategy string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n%s: %s\\n\", message, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)\n}\n\n// FormatTaskMetadata creates a commit message with task metadata trailer.\nfunc FormatTaskMetadata(message, taskMetadataDir string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n%s: %s\\n\", message, MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataDir)\n}\n\n// FormatTaskMetadataWithStrategy creates a commit message with task metadata and strategy trailers.\nfunc FormatTaskMetadataWithStrategy(message, taskMetadataDir, strategy string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n%s: %s\\n%s: %s\\n\", message, MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataDir, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)\n}\n\n// FormatSourceRef creates a formatted source ref string for the trailer.\n// Format: \"<branch>@<commit-hash-prefix>\" (hash truncated to 12 chars)\nfunc FormatSourceRef(branch, commitHash string) string {\n\tshortHash := commitHash\n\tif len(shortHash) > 12 {\n\t\tshortHash = shortHash[:12]\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s@%s\", branch, shortHash)\n}\n\n// FormatMetadata creates a commit message with metadata trailer.\nfunc FormatMetadata(message, metadataDir string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n%s: %s\\n\", message, MetadataTrailerKey, metadataDir)\n}\n\n// FormatMetadataWithStrategy creates a commit message with metadata and strategy trailers.\nfunc FormatMetadataWithStrategy(message, metadataDir, strategy string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n\\n%s: %s\\n%s: %s\\n\", message, MetadataTrailerKey, metadataDir, StrategyTrailerKey, strategy)\n}\n\n// FormatShadowCommit creates a commit message for manual-commit strategy checkpoints.\n// Includes Entire-Metadata, Entire-Session, and Entire-Strategy trailers.\nfunc FormatShadowCommit(message, metadataDir, sessionID string) string {\n\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\tsb.WriteString(message)\n\tsb.WriteString(\"\\n\\n\")\n\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s: %s\\n\", MetadataTrailerKey, metadataDir))\n\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s: %s\\n\", SessionTrailerKey, sessionID))\n\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s: %s\\n\", StrategyTrailerKey, \"manual-commit\"))\n\treturn sb.String()\n}\n\n// FormatShadowTaskCommit creates a commit message for manual-commit task checkpoints.\n// Includes Entire-Metadata-Task, Entire-Session, and Entire-Strategy trailers.\nfunc FormatShadowTaskCommit(message, taskMetadataDir, sessionID string) string {\n\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\tsb.WriteString(message)\n\tsb.WriteString(\"\\n\\n\")\n\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s: %s\\n\", MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataDir))\n\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s: %s\\n\", SessionTrailerKey, sessionID))\n\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s: %s\\n\", StrategyTrailerKey, \"manual-commit\"))\n\treturn sb.String()\n}\n"
}

    25→// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/sessions branch.
    26→// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    27→//
    28→// For task checkpoints (IsTask=true), additional files are written under tasks/<tool-use-id>/:
    29→//   - For incremental checkpoints: checkpoints/NNN-<tool-use-id>.json
    30→//   - For final checkpoints: checkpoint.json and agent-<agent-id>.jsonl
    31→func (s *GitStore) WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error {
    32→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
    33→
    34→	// Validate identifiers to prevent path traversal and malformed data
    35→	if err := trailers.ValidateCheckpointID(opts.CheckpointID); err != nil {
    36→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→	if err := paths.ValidateToolUseID(opts.ToolUseID); err != nil {
    42→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→	if err := paths.ValidateAgentID(opts.AgentID); err != nil {
    45→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
    49→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
    50→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
    54→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		return err
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	// Use sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    60→	basePath := trailers.CheckpointPath(opts.CheckpointID) + "/"
    61→
    62→	// Track task metadata path for commit trailer
    63→	var taskMetadataPath string
    64→
    65→	// Handle task checkpoints
    66→	if opts.IsTask && opts.ToolUseID != "" {
    67→		taskMetadataPath, err = s.writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries)
    68→		if err != nil {
    69→			return err
    70→		}

    25→// isNotFoundError checks if an error represents a "not found" condition in go-git.
    26→// This includes entry not found, file not found, directory not found, and object not found.
    27→func isNotFoundError(err error) bool {
    28→	return errors.Is(err, object.ErrEntryNotFound) ||
    29→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrFileNotFound) ||
    30→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrDirectoryNotFound) ||
    31→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrObjectNotFound) ||
    32→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound)
    33→}
    34→
    35→// commitOrHead attempts to create a commit. If the commit would be empty (files already
    36→// committed), it returns HEAD hash instead. This handles the case where files were
    37→// modified during a session but already committed by the user before the hook runs.
    38→func commitOrHead(repo *git.Repository, worktree *git.Worktree, msg string, author *object.Signature) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
    39→	commitHash, err := worktree.Commit(msg, &git.CommitOptions{Author: author})
    40→	if errors.Is(err, git.ErrEmptyCommit) {
    41→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No changes to commit (files already committed)\n")
    42→		head, err := repo.Head()
    43→		if err != nil {
    44→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    45→		}
    46→		return head.Hash(), nil
    47→	}
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to commit: %w", err)
    50→	}

    21→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
    22→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    23→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    24→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    25→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    26→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    27→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    28→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    31→		return defaultYes
    32→	}
    33→	defer tty.Close()
    34→
    35→	// Show context if provided
    36→	if context != "" {
    37→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	// Show prompt with default indicator

   285→func TestNewCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {
   286→	tests := []struct {

   296→		{
   297→			name:    "too short",

   299→			wantErr: true,
   300→		},
   301→		{
   302→			name:    "too long",
   303→			input:   "a1b2c3d4e5f6789012",
   304→			wantErr: true,
   305→		},
   306→		{
   307→			name:    "non-hex characters",
   308→			input:   "a1b2c3d4e5gg",

   314→			wantErr: true,
   315→		},
